include:
  - project: 'devops/gitlab-ci'
    ref: master
    file: '/deploy_image/.gitlab-ci_deploy_script.yml'
  - project: 'devops/gitlab-ci'
    ref: master
    file: '/reload_proxy/.gitlab-ci_reload_proxy_script.yml'

variables:
  #build params
  BUILD_IMAGE: 'node:14-alpine'
  #test params
  TEST_IMAGE: 'gitlab.nitrowise.com:8090/docker/node14-with-chromium:latest'
  # publish params
  PUBLISH_IMAGE: 'docker:stable'
  DOCKER_BUILD_EXTRA_ARGS: ''
  DEBUG_PUBLISH: 0
  DEBUG_RELOAD_PROXY: 0
  DOCKERFILE: './docker/Dockerfile'
  DOCKER_REGISTRY: 'gitlab.nitrowise.com:8090'

  ### DEPLOY PARAMETERS ###
  TEST_APP_NAME: 'peter-kepkeret-html'
  STAGE_APP_NAME: 'peter-kepkeret-html'
  PROD_APP_NAME: 'peter-kepkeret-html'

  REGISTRY_IMAGE_TO_DEPLOY: "gitlab-registry.nitrowise.com/peter-kepkeret/peter-kepkeret-html"
  PROD_PROXY_CONTAINER: "pk-vps-nginx"
  PROD_CONTAINER_NAME: "peter-kepkeret-html-prod"
  PROD_DEPLOY_TO_SERVERS: "185.208.225.182"
  PROD_DEPLOY_TO_SERVERS_SSH_PORT: "4"
  PROD_SERVER_DOCKER_NETWORK: "pk-vps-prod-net"
  PROD_SERVER_CHECK_DOCKER_CMD: "/opt/devops-tools/SYS_scripts/docker-scripts/docker_container_startup_wait_to_stable_stat.sh"
  PROD_DOCKER_RUN_EXTRA_ARGS: ""

cache:
  paths:
    - node_modules/

stages:
  - build
  - publish
  - deploy
  - deploy-fallback
  - reload-proxy

build-test:
  stage: build
  image: ${BUILD_IMAGE}
  only:
    - develop
    - stage
    - /^feature.*$/
  tags:
    - dockered-runner
  artifacts:
    paths:
      - dist/
    expire_in: 1 day
  script:
    - npm install --silent
    - npm run build-test

build-prod:
  stage: build
  image: ${BUILD_IMAGE}
  only:
    - master
    - tags
  tags:
    - dockered-runner
  artifacts:
    paths:
      - dist/
    expire_in: 1 day
  script:
    - npm install --silent
    - npm run build

publish-nonprod:
  stage: publish
  image: ${PUBLISH_IMAGE}
  only:
    - develop
    - /^feature.*$/
    - stage
  tags:
    - dood
  services:
    - docker:dind
  cache: {}
  script:
    - docker login -u gitlab-ci-token -p $CI_JOB_TOKEN $CI_REGISTRY
    - if [ ${DEBUG_PUBLISH} -ne 0 ] ; then sleep 10000 ; fi
    - docker build --pull --file ${DOCKERFILE} ${DOCKER_BUILD_EXTRA_ARGS}
      -t "${DOCKER_REGISTRY}/${CI_PROJECT_PATH}:${CI_COMMIT_REF_NAME}-${CI_COMMIT_SHORT_SHA}"
      -t "${DOCKER_REGISTRY}/${CI_PROJECT_PATH}:${CI_COMMIT_REF_NAME}-latest"
      .
    - docker push ${DOCKER_REGISTRY}/${CI_PROJECT_PATH}
    - docker rmi "${DOCKER_REGISTRY}/${CI_PROJECT_PATH}:${CI_COMMIT_REF_NAME}-latest"
    - docker rmi "${DOCKER_REGISTRY}/${CI_PROJECT_PATH}:${CI_COMMIT_REF_NAME}-${CI_COMMIT_SHORT_SHA}"

publish-prod:
  stage: publish
  image: ${PUBLISH_IMAGE}
  only:
    - tags
  tags:
    - dood
  services:
    - docker:dind
  cache: {}
  script:
    - docker login -u gitlab-ci-token -p $CI_JOB_TOKEN $CI_REGISTRY
    - docker build --pull --file ${DOCKERFILE} ${DOCKER_BUILD_EXTRA_ARGS}
      -t "${DOCKER_REGISTRY}/${CI_PROJECT_PATH}:${CI_COMMIT_TAG}"
      -t "${DOCKER_REGISTRY}/${CI_PROJECT_PATH}:latest"
      .
    - docker push ${DOCKER_REGISTRY}/${CI_PROJECT_PATH}
    - docker rmi "${DOCKER_REGISTRY}/${CI_PROJECT_PATH}:latest"
    - docker rmi "${DOCKER_REGISTRY}/${CI_PROJECT_PATH}:${CI_COMMIT_TAG}"

deploy-prod:
  stage: deploy
  image: gitlab.nitrowise.com:8090/docker/runner-sshremote:latest
  only:
    - tags
  variables:
    # DEPLOYED_TAG_VARIABLE: name of Gitlab CI variable which contains the version information
    # Why to use a variable to store the name of the deployed tag variable?
    #   Because while calling Gitlab API, we need to use both the name and the value of the variable
    # Reaching the value of $PROD_DEPLOYED_TAG: ${!DEPLOYED_TAG_VARIABLE}
    DEPLOYED_TAG_VARIABLE: "PROD_DEPLOYED_TAG"
  before_script:
    - |
      export _conf_DEPLOY_TO_SERVERS=${PROD_DEPLOY_TO_SERVERS}
      export _conf_DEPLOY_TO_SERVERS_SSH_PORT=${PROD_DEPLOY_TO_SERVERS_SSH_PORT}
      export _conf_CONTAINER_NAME=${PROD_CONTAINER_NAME}
      export _conf_SERVER_DOCKER_NETWORK=${PROD_SERVER_DOCKER_NETWORK}
      export _conf_DOCKER_RUN_EXTRA_ARGS=${PROD_DOCKER_RUN_EXTRA_ARGS}
      export _conf_CHECK_DOCKER_CMD=${PROD_SERVER_CHECK_DOCKER_CMD}
      export _conf_TAG_TO_DEPLOY="${CI_COMMIT_TAG}"
  script:
    #- !reference [.deploy_check_api_token, script]
    - !reference [.deploy_script, script]
    - !reference [.deploy_update_deployed_tag_var, script]

deploy-fallback-prod:
  needs: ["deploy-prod"]
  stage: deploy-fallback
  image: gitlab.nitrowise.com:8090/docker/runner-sshremote:latest
  only:
    - tags
  when: on_failure
  variables:
    DEPLOYED_TAG_VARIABLE: "PROD_DEPLOYED_TAG"
  before_script:
    - |
      export _conf_DEPLOY_TO_SERVERS=${PROD_DEPLOY_TO_SERVERS}
      export _conf_DEPLOY_TO_SERVERS_SSH_PORT=${PROD_DEPLOY_TO_SERVERS_SSH_PORT}
      export _conf_CONTAINER_NAME=${PROD_CONTAINER_NAME}
      export _conf_SERVER_DOCKER_NETWORK=${PROD_SERVER_DOCKER_NETWORK}
      export _conf_DOCKER_RUN_EXTRA_ARGS=${PROD_DOCKER_RUN_EXTRA_ARGS}
      export _conf_CHECK_DOCKER_CMD=${PROD_SERVER_CHECK_DOCKER_CMD}
      export _conf_TAG_TO_DEPLOY="${!DEPLOYED_TAG_VARIABLE}"
      export _conf_DEPLOY_STAGE="deploy-prod"
  script:
    #- !reference [.deploy_check_api_token, script]
    - !reference [.deploy_fallback_check_linked_jobs, script]
    - !reference [.deploy_script, script]

reload-prod-proxy:
  stage: reload-proxy
  image: gitlab.nitrowise.com:8090/docker/runner-sshremote:latest
  only:
    - tags
  before_script:
    - export _conf_PROXY_SERVERS="${PROD_DEPLOY_TO_SERVERS}"
      export _conf_PROXY_SERVERS_SSH_PORT=${PROD_DEPLOY_TO_SERVERS_SSH_PORT}
    - export _conf_PROXY_CONTAINER_NAME="${PROD_PROXY_CONTAINER}"
  script:
    - !reference [.reload_proxy_debug, script]
    - !reference [.reload_frontend_proxy, script]

// Karma configuration file, see link for more information
// https://karma-runner.github.io/1.0/config/configuration-file.html

module.exports = function (config) {
  config.set({
    basePath: '',
    frameworks: ['jasmine', '@angular-devkit/build-angular'],
    plugins: [
      require('karma-jasmine'),
      require('karma-chrome-launcher'),
      require('karma-jasmine-html-reporter'),
      require('karma-coverage'),
      require('@angular-devkit/build-angular/plugins/karma')
    ],
    client: {
      jasmine: {
        // you can add configuration options for Jasmine here
        // the possible options are listed at https://jasmine.github.io/api/edge/Configuration.html
        // for example, you can disable the random execution with `random: false`
        // or set a specific seed with `seed: 4321`
      },
      clearContext: false // leave Jasmine Spec Runner output visible in browser
    },
    jasmineHtmlReporter: {
      suppressAll: true // removes the duplicated traces
    },
    coverageReporter: {
      dir: require('path').join(__dirname, './coverage/peter-kepkeret-html'),
      subdir: '.',
      reporters: [
        { type: 'html' },
        { type: 'text-summary' }
      ]
    },
    reporters: ['progress', 'kjhtml'],
    port: 9876,
    colors: true,
    logLevel: config.LOG_INFO,
    autoWatch: true,
    browsers: ['Chrome'],
    singleRun: false,
    restartOnFileChange: true
  });
};

📄 FILE: README.md

# PeterKepkeretHtml

This project was generated with [Angular CLI](https://github.com/angular/angular-cli) version 14.0.4.

## Development server

Run `ng serve` for a dev server. Navigate to `http://localhost:4200/`. The application will automatically reload if you change any of the source files.

## Code scaffolding

Run `ng generate component component-name` to generate a new component. You can also use `ng generate directive|pipe|service|class|guard|interface|enum|module`.

## Build

Run `ng build` to build the project. The build artifacts will be stored in the `dist/` directory.

## Running unit tests

Run `ng test` to execute the unit tests via [Karma](https://karma-runner.github.io).

## Running end-to-end tests

Run `ng e2e` to execute the end-to-end tests via a platform of your choice. To use this command, you need to first add a package that implements end-to-end testing capabilities.

## Further help

To get more help on the Angular CLI use `ng help` or go check out the [Angular CLI Overview and Command Reference](https://angular.io/cli) page.

import { enableProdMode } from '@angular/core';
import { platformBrowserDynamic } from '@angular/platform-browser-dynamic';

import { AppModule } from './app/app.module';
import { environment } from './environments/environment';

if (environment.production) {
  enableProdMode();
}

platformBrowserDynamic().bootstrapModule(AppModule)
  .catch(err => console.error(err));

@use '@angular/material' as mat;

// Include the common styles for Angular Material. We include this here so that you only
// have to load a single css file for Angular Material in your app.

// Fonts
@import 'https://fonts.googleapis.com/icon?family=Material+Icons';
@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Roboto:wght@300;400;500&display=swap');

$fontConfig: (
  display-4: mat.define-typography-level(112px, 112px, 300, 'Roboto', -0.0134em),
  display-3: mat.define-typography-level(56px, 56px, 400, 'Roboto', -0.0089em),
  display-2: mat.define-typography-level(45px, 48px, 400, 'Roboto', 0.0000em),
  display-1: mat.define-typography-level(34px, 40px, 400, 'Roboto', 0.0074em),
  headline: mat.define-typography-level(24px, 32px, 400, 'Roboto', 0.0000em),
  title: mat.define-typography-level(20px, 32px, 500, 'Roboto', 0.0075em),

📄 FILE: src\polyfills.ts

/**
 * This file includes polyfills needed by Angular and is loaded before the app.
 * You can add your own extra polyfills to this file.
 *
 * This file is divided into 2 sections:
 *   1. Browser polyfills. These are applied before loading ZoneJS and are sorted by browsers.
 *   2. Application imports. Files imported after ZoneJS that should be loaded before your main
 *      file.
 *
 * The current setup is for so-called "evergreen" browsers; the last versions of browsers that
 * automatically update themselves. This includes recent versions of Safari, Chrome (including
 * Opera), Edge on the desktop, and iOS and Chrome on mobile.
 *
 * Learn more in https://angular.io/guide/browser-support
 */

/***************************************************************************************************
 * BROWSER POLYFILLS
 */

/**
 * By default, zone.js will patch all possible macroTask and DomEvents
 * user can disable parts of macroTask/DomEvents patch by setting following flags
 * because those flags need to be set before `zone.js` being loaded, and webpack
 * will put import in the top of bundle, so user need to create a separate file
 * in this directory (for example: zone-flags.ts), and put the following flags
 * into that file, and then add the following code before importing zone.js.
 * import './zone-flags';
 *
 * The flags allowed in zone-flags.ts are listed here.
 *
 * The following flags will work for all browsers.
 *
 * (window as any).__Zone_disable_requestAnimationFrame = true; // disable patch requestAnimationFrame
 * (window as any).__Zone_disable_on_property = true; // disable patch onProperty such as onclick
 * (window as any).__zone_symbol__UNPATCHED_EVENTS = ['scroll', 'mousemove']; // disable patch specified eventNames
 *
 *  in IE/Edge developer tools, the addEventListener will also be wrapped by zone.js
 *  with the following flag, it will bypass `zone.js` patch for IE/Edge
 *
 *  (window as any).__Zone_enable_cross_context_check = true;
 *
 */

/***************************************************************************************************
 * Zone JS is required by default for Angular itself.
 */
import 'zone.js';  // Included with Angular CLI.


/***************************************************************************************************
 * APPLICATION IMPORTS
 */

// This file is required by karma.conf.js and loads recursively all the .spec and framework files

import 'zone.js/testing';
import { getTestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import {
  BrowserDynamicTestingModule,
  platformBrowserDynamicTesting
} from '@angular/platform-browser-dynamic/testing';

declare const require: {
  context(path: string, deep?: boolean, filter?: RegExp): {
    <T>(id: string): T;
    keys(): string[];
  };
};

// First, initialize the Angular testing environment.
getTestBed().initTestEnvironment(
  BrowserDynamicTestingModule,
  platformBrowserDynamicTesting(),
);

// Then we find all the tests.
const context = require.context('./', true, /\.spec\.ts$/);
// And load the modules.
context.keys().forEach(context);

import {NgModule} from '@angular/core';
import {RouterModule, Routes} from '@angular/router';

const appRoutes: Routes = [
  {
    path: 'dashboard',
    loadChildren: () => import('./features/dashboard/dashboard.module').then(m => m.DashboardModule),
    canActivate: []
  },
  {
    path: 'users',
    loadChildren: () => import('./features/users/users.module').then(m => m.UsersModule),
    canActivate: []
  },
  {
    path: 'cikkcsoportok',
    loadChildren: () => import('./features/cikkcsoport/cikkcsoport.module').then(m => m.CikkcsoportModule),
    canActivate: []
  },
  {
    path: 'arucikkek',
    loadChildren: () => import('./features/arucikk/arucikk.module').then(m => m.ArucikkModule),
    canActivate: []
  },
  {
    path: 'keszletezes',
    loadChildren: () => import('./features/keszletezes/keszletezes.module').then(m => m.KeszletezesModule),
    canActivate: []
  },
  {
    path: 'torzsadatok',
    loadChildren: () => import('./features/altalanos-adatok/torzsadat.module').then(m => m.TorzsadatModule),
    canActivate: []
  },
  {
    path: 'megrendeles',
    loadChildren: () => import('./features/megrendeles/megrendeles.module').then(m => m.MegrendelesModule),
    canActivate: []
  },
  {
    path: 'vevok',
    loadChildren: () => import('./features/vevok/vevok.module').then(m => m.VevokModule),
    canActivate: []
  },
  {
    path: 'sablonok',
    loadChildren: () => import('./features/sablonok/sablonok.module').then(m => m.SablonokModule),
    canActivate: []
  },
  {
    path: '**',
    redirectTo: 'dashboard',
    pathMatch: 'full'
  }
];

@NgModule({
  imports: [
    RouterModule.forRoot(appRoutes)
  ],
  exports: [RouterModule],
  providers: []
})
export class AppRoutingModule { }

📄 FILE: src\app\api\api.module.ts
====================================================================================================

/* tslint:disable */
/* eslint-disable */
import { NgModule, ModuleWithProviders, SkipSelf, Optional } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { ApiConfiguration, ApiConfigurationParams } from './api-configuration';

import { VevoService } from './services/vevo.service';
import { TorzsadatService } from './services/torzsadat.service';
import { TetelcsoportService } from './services/tetelcsoport.service';
import { TetelService } from './services/tetel.service';
import { SablonService } from './services/sablon.service';
import { MegrendelesService } from './services/megrendeles.service';
import { DolgozoService } from './services/dolgozo.service';
import { CikkcsoportService } from './services/cikkcsoport.service';
import { ArucikkService } from './services/arucikk.service';
import { KeszletmozgasService } from './services/keszletmozgas.service';
import { EladasService } from './services/eladas.service';
import { NyugtaService } from './services/nyugta.service';
import { MegrendelesPdfService } from './services/megrendeles-pdf.service';
import { GyartasiLapPdfService } from './services/gyartasi-lap-pdf.service';

/**
 * Module that provides all services and configuration.
 */
@NgModule({
  imports: [],
  exports: [],
  declarations: [],
  providers: [
    VevoService,
    TorzsadatService,
    TetelcsoportService,
    TetelService,
    SablonService,
    MegrendelesService,
    DolgozoService,
    CikkcsoportService,
    ArucikkService,
    KeszletmozgasService,
    EladasService,
    NyugtaService,
    MegrendelesPdfService,
    GyartasiLapPdfService,
    ApiConfiguration
  ],
})
export class ApiModule {
  static forRoot(params: ApiConfigurationParams): ModuleWithProviders<ApiModule> {
    return {
      ngModule: ApiModule,
      providers: [
        {
          provide: ApiConfiguration,
          useValue: params
        }
      ]
    }
  }

  constructor( 
    @Optional() @SkipSelf() parentModule: ApiModule,
    @Optional() http: HttpClient
  ) {
    if (parentModule) {
      throw new Error('ApiModule is already loaded. Import in your base AppModule only.');
    }
    if (!http) {
      throw new Error('You need to import the HttpClientModule in your AppModule! \n' +
      'See also https://github.com/angular/angular/issues/20575');
    }
  }
}


====================================================================================================
📄 FILE: src\app\api\base-service.ts
====================================================================================================

/* tslint:disable */
/* eslint-disable */
import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { ApiConfiguration } from './api-configuration';

/**
 * Base class for services
 */
@Injectable()
export class BaseService {
  constructor(
    protected config: ApiConfiguration,
    protected http: HttpClient
  ) {
  }

  private _rootUrl: string = '';

  /**
   * Returns the root url for all operations in this service. If not set directly in this
   * service, will fallback to `ApiConfiguration.rootUrl`.
   */
  get rootUrl(): string {
    return this._rootUrl || this.config.rootUrl;
  }

  /**
   * Sets the root URL for API operations in this service.
   */
  set rootUrl(rootUrl: string) {
    this._rootUrl = rootUrl;
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\api\request-builder.ts

/* tslint:disable */
/* eslint-disable */
import { HttpRequest, HttpParameterCodec, HttpParams, HttpHeaders, HttpContext } from '@angular/common/http';

/**
 * Custom parameter codec to correctly handle the plus sign in parameter
 * values. See https://github.com/angular/angular/issues/18261
 */
class ParameterCodec implements HttpParameterCodec {
  encodeKey(key: string): string {
    return encodeURIComponent(key);
  }

  encodeValue(value: string): string {
    return encodeURIComponent(value);
  }

  decodeKey(key: string): string {
    return decodeURIComponent(key);
  }

  decodeValue(value: string): string {
    return decodeURIComponent(value);
  }
}
const ParameterCodecInstance = new ParameterCodec();

/**
 * Defines the options for appending a parameter
 */
interface ParameterOptions {
  style?: string;
  explode?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Base class for a parameter
 */
abstract class Parameter {
  constructor(public name: string, public value: any, public options: ParameterOptions, defaultStyle: string, defaultExplode: boolean) {
    this.options = options || {};
    if (this.options.style === null || this.options.style === undefined) {
      this.options.style = defaultStyle;
    }
    if (this.options.explode === null || this.options.explode === undefined) {
      this.options.explode = defaultExplode;
    }
  }

  serializeValue(value: any, separator = ','): string {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) {
      return '';
    } else if (value instanceof Array) {
      return value.map(v => this.serializeValue(v).split(separator).join(encodeURIComponent(separator))).join(separator);
    } else if (typeof value === 'object') {
      const array: string[] = [];
      for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
        let propVal = value[key];
        if (propVal !== null && propVal !== undefined) {
          propVal = this.serializeValue(propVal).split(separator).join(encodeURIComponent(separator));
          if (this.options.explode) {
            array.push(`${key}=${propVal}`);
          } else {
            array.push(key);
            array.push(propVal);
          }
        }
      }
      return array.join(separator);
    } else {
      return String(value);
    }
  }
}

/**
 * A parameter in the operation path
 */
class PathParameter extends Parameter {
  constructor(name: string, value: any, options: ParameterOptions) {
    super(name, value, options, 'simple', false);
  }

  append(path: string): string {
    let value = this.value;
    if (value === null || value === undefined) {
      value = '';
    }
    let prefix = this.options.style === 'label' ? '.' : '';
    let separator = this.options.explode ? prefix === '' ? ',' : prefix : ',';
    let alreadySerialized = false;
    if (this.options.style === 'matrix') {
      // The parameter name is just used as prefix, except in some cases...
      prefix = `;${this.name}=`;
      if (this.options.explode && typeof value === 'object') {
        prefix = ';';
        if (value instanceof Array) {
          // For arrays we have to repeat the name for each element
          value = value.map(v => `${this.name}=${this.serializeValue(v, ';')}`);
          value = value.join(';');
          alreadySerialized = true;
        } else {
          // For objects we have to put each the key / value pairs
          value = this.serializeValue(value, ';');
          alreadySerialized = true
        }
      }
    }
    value = prefix + (alreadySerialized ? value : this.serializeValue(value, separator));
    // Replace both the plain variable and the corresponding variant taking in the prefix and explode into account
    path = path.replace(`{${this.name}}`, value);
    path = path.replace(`{${prefix}${this.name}${this.options.explode ? '*' : ''}}`, value);
    return path;
  }

  // @ts-ignore
  serializeValue(value: any, separator = ','): string {
    var result = typeof value === 'string' ? encodeURIComponent(value) : super.serializeValue(value, separator);
    result = result.replace(/%3D/g, '=');
    result = result.replace(/%3B/g, ';');
    result = result.replace(/%2C/g, ',');
    return result;
  }
}

/**
 * A parameter in the query
 */
class QueryParameter extends Parameter {
  constructor(name: string, value: any, options: ParameterOptions) {
    super(name, value, options, 'form', true);
  }

  append(params: HttpParams): HttpParams {
    if (this.value instanceof Array) {
      // Array serialization
      if (this.options.explode) {
        for (const v of this.value) {
          params = params.append(this.name, this.serializeValue(v));
        }
      } else {
        const separator = this.options.style === 'spaceDelimited'
          ? ' ' : this.options.style === 'pipeDelimited'
            ? '|' : ',';
        return params.append(this.name, this.serializeValue(this.value, separator));
      }
    } else if (this.value !== null && typeof this.value === 'object') {
      // Object serialization
      if (this.options.style === 'deepObject') {
        // Append a parameter for each key, in the form `name[key]`
        for (const key of Object.keys(this.value)) {
          const propVal = this.value[key];
          if (propVal !== null && propVal !== undefined) {
            params = params.append(`${this.name}[${key}]`, this.serializeValue(propVal));
          }
        }
      } else if (this.options.explode) {
        // Append a parameter for each key without using the parameter name
        for (const key of Object.keys(this.value)) {
          const propVal = this.value[key];
          if (propVal !== null && propVal !== undefined) {
            params = params.append(key, this.serializeValue(propVal));
          }
        }
      } else {
        // Append a single parameter whose values are a comma-separated list of key,value,key,value...
        const array: any[] = [];
        for (const key of Object.keys(this.value)) {
          const propVal = this.value[key];
          if (propVal !== null && propVal !== undefined) {
            array.push(key);
            array.push(propVal);
          }
        }
        params = params.append(this.name, this.serializeValue(array));
      }
    } else if (this.value !== null && this.value !== undefined) {
      // Plain value
      params = params.append(this.name, this.serializeValue(this.value));
    }
    return params;
  }
}

/**
 * A parameter in the HTTP request header
 */
class HeaderParameter extends Parameter {
  constructor(name: string, value: any, options: ParameterOptions) {
    super(name, value, options, 'simple', false);
  }

  append(headers: HttpHeaders): HttpHeaders {
    if (this.value !== null && this.value !== undefined) {
      if (this.value instanceof Array) {
        for (const v of this.value) {
          headers = headers.append(this.name, this.serializeValue(v));
        }
      } else {
        headers = headers.append(this.name, this.serializeValue(this.value));
      }
    }
    return headers;
  }
}

/**
 * Helper to build http requests from parameters
 */
export class RequestBuilder {

  private _path = new Map<string, PathParameter>();
  private _query = new Map<string, QueryParameter>();
  private _header = new Map<string, HeaderParameter>();
  _bodyContent: any | null;
  _bodyContentType?: string;

  constructor(
    public rootUrl: string,
    public operationPath: string,
    public method: string) {
  }

  /**
   * Sets a path parameter
   */
  path(name: string, value: any, options?: ParameterOptions): void {
    this._path.set(name, new PathParameter(name, value, options || {}));
  }

  /**
   * Sets a query parameter
   */
  query(name: string, value: any, options?: ParameterOptions): void {
    this._query.set(name, new QueryParameter(name, value, options || {}));
  }

  /**
   * Sets a header parameter
   */
  header(name: string, value: any, options?: ParameterOptions): void {
    this._header.set(name, new HeaderParameter(name, value, options || {}));
  }

  /**
   * Sets the body content, along with the content type
   */
  body(value: any, contentType = 'application/json'): void {
    if (value instanceof Blob) {
      this._bodyContentType = value.type;
    } else {
      this._bodyContentType = contentType;
    }
    if (this._bodyContentType === 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' && value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
      // Handle URL-encoded data
      const pairs: Array<[string, string]> = [];
      for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
        let val = value[key];
        if (!(val instanceof Array)) {
          val = [val];
        }
        for (const v of val) {
          const formValue = this.formDataValue(v);
          if (formValue !== null) {
            pairs.push([key, formValue]);
          }
        }
      }
      this._bodyContent = pairs.map(p => `${encodeURIComponent(p[0])}=${encodeURIComponent(p[1])}`).join('&');
    } else if (this._bodyContentType === 'multipart/form-data') {
      // Handle multipart form data
      const formData = new FormData();
      if (value !== null && value !== undefined) {
        for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
          const val = value[key];
          if (val instanceof Array) {
            for (const v of val) {
              const toAppend = this.formDataValue(v);
              if (toAppend !== null) {
                formData.append(key, toAppend);
              }
            }
          } else {
            const toAppend = this.formDataValue(val);
            if (toAppend !== null) {
              formData.set(key, toAppend);
            }
          }
        }
      }
      this._bodyContent = formData;
    } else {
      // The body is the plain content
      this._bodyContent = value;
    }
  }

  private formDataValue(value: any): any {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) {
      return null;
    }
    if (value instanceof Blob) {
      return value;
    }
    if (typeof value === 'object') {
      return JSON.stringify(value);
    }
    return String(value);
  }

  /**
   * Builds the request with the current set parameters
   */
  build<T = any>(options?: {
    /** Which content types to accept */
    accept?: string;

    /** The expected response type */
    responseType?: 'json' | 'text' | 'blob' | 'arraybuffer';

    /** Whether to report progress on uploads / downloads */
    reportProgress?: boolean;

    /** Allow passing HttpContext for HttpClient */
    context?: HttpContext;
  }): HttpRequest<T> {

    options = options || {};

    // Path parameters
    let path = this.operationPath;
    for (const pathParam of this._path.values()) {
      path = pathParam.append(path);
    }
    const url = this.rootUrl + path;

    // Query parameters
    let httpParams = new HttpParams({
      encoder: ParameterCodecInstance
    });
    for (const queryParam of this._query.values()) {
      httpParams = queryParam.append(httpParams);
    }

    // Header parameters
    let httpHeaders = new HttpHeaders();
    if (options.accept) {
      httpHeaders = httpHeaders.append('Accept', options.accept);
    }
    for (const headerParam of this._header.values()) {
      httpHeaders = headerParam.append(httpHeaders);
    }

    // Request content headers
    if (this._bodyContentType && !(this._bodyContent instanceof FormData)) {
      httpHeaders = httpHeaders.set('Content-Type', this._bodyContentType);
    }

    // Perform the request
    return new HttpRequest<T>(this.method.toUpperCase(), url, this._bodyContent, {
      params: httpParams,
      headers: httpHeaders,
      responseType: options.responseType,
      reportProgress: options.reportProgress,
      context: options.context
    });
  }
}

/* tslint:disable */
/* eslint-disable */
import { HttpResponse } from '@angular/common/http';

/**
 * Constrains the http response to not have the body defined as `T | null`, but `T` only.
 */
export type StrictHttpResponse<T> = HttpResponse<T> & {
  readonly body: T;
}

/* tslint:disable */

import { EgyediKepkeret } from './egyedi-kepkeret';
import { Tetel } from './tetel';
export interface EgyediKepkeretTetel {
  egyediKepkeret?: EgyediKepkeret;
  id?: number;
  idoSzorzo?: number;
  letrehozas?: string;
  megjegyzes?: string;
  szamitottAr?: number;

  }

   *
   * This method provides access to the full `HttpResponse`, allowing access to response headers.
   * To access only the response body, use `getArucikk()` instead.
   *
   * This method doesn't expect any request body.
   */
  getArucikk$Response(params: {
    id: number;
    context?: HttpContext
  }
): Observable<StrictHttpResponse<ArucikkItemDto>> {

    const rb = new RequestBuilder(this.rootUrl, ArucikkService.GetArucikkPath, 'get');
    if (params) {
      rb.path('id', params.id, {});
    }

    return this.http.request(rb.build({
      responseType: 'json',
      accept: 'application/json',
      context: params?.context
    })).pipe(
      filter((r: any) => r instanceof HttpResponse),
      map((r: HttpResponse<any>) => {
        return r as StrictHttpResponse<ArucikkItemDto>;
      })
    );
  }

    /**
     * Zero-based page index (0..N)
     */
    page?: number;

     */
    size?: number;

     * Sorting criteria in the format: property(,asc|desc). Default sort order is ascending. Multiple sort criteria are supported.
     */
    sort?: Array<string>;
    datumTol?: string;
    datumIg?: string;
    vevoId?: number;
    bizonylatszam?: string;
    dolgozoId?: number;
    context?: HttpContext
  }
): Observable<StrictHttpResponse<PageEladasDto>> {

      rb.query('page', params.page, {});
      rb.query('size', params.size, {});

      rb.query('datumIg', params.datumIg, {});
      rb.query('vevoId', params.vevoId, {});
      rb.query('bizonylatszam', params.bizonylatszam, {});
      rb.query('dolgozoId', params.dolgozoId, {});

   * To access only the response body, use `generateMegrendelesPdf()` instead.
   *

  generateMegrendelesPdf$Response(params: {
    id: number;
    context?: HttpContext
  }

      rb.query('id', params.id, {});
    }

    return this.http.request(rb.build({
      responseType: 'text',
      accept: '*/*',
      context: params?.context
    })).pipe(
      filter((r: any) => r instanceof HttpResponse),
      map((r: HttpResponse<any>) => {
        return (r as HttpResponse<any>).clone({ body: undefined }) as StrictHttpResponse<void>;
      })
    );
  }

  /**

  }

import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})

  constructor(
    config: ApiConfiguration,
    http: HttpClient
  ) {
    super(config, http);
  }

  /**
   * Path part for operation generateNyugtaPdf
   */
  static readonly GenerateNyugtaPdfPath = '/api/nyugta';

  /**
   * Pdf nyugtát generál az adott eladáshoz.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to the full `HttpResponse`, allowing access to response headers.
   * To access only the response body, use `generateNyugtaPdf()` instead.
   *
   * This method doesn't expect any request body.
   */
  generateNyugtaPdf$Response(params: {
    eladasId: number;
    context?: HttpContext
  }
): Observable<StrictHttpResponse<void>> {

    const rb = new RequestBuilder(this.rootUrl, NyugtaService.GenerateNyugtaPdfPath, 'get');
    if (params) {
      rb.query('eladasId', params.eladasId, {});
    }

    return this.http.request(rb.build({
      responseType: 'text',
      accept: '*/*',
      context: params?.context
    })).pipe(
      filter((r: any) => r instanceof HttpResponse),
      map((r: HttpResponse<any>) => {
        return (r as HttpResponse<any>).clone({ body: undefined }) as StrictHttpResponse<void>;
      })
    );
  }

  /**
   * Pdf nyugtát generál az adott eladáshoz.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to only to the response body.
   * To access the full response (for headers, for example), `generateNyugtaPdf$Response()` instead.
   *
   * This method doesn't expect any request body.

  generateNyugtaPdf(params: {
    eladasId: number;
    context?: HttpContext
  }
): Observable<void> {

    return this.generateNyugtaPdf$Response(params).pipe(
      map((r: StrictHttpResponse<void>) => r.body as void)

}

/* tslint:disable */

import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpClient, HttpResponse, HttpContext } from '@angular/common/http';
import { BaseService } from '../base-service';
import { ApiConfiguration } from '../api-configuration';
import { StrictHttpResponse } from '../strict-http-response';
import { RequestBuilder } from '../request-builder';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { map, filter } from 'rxjs/operators';

import { PageVevoDto } from '../models/page-vevo-dto';
import { VevoDto } from '../models/vevo-dto';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root',
})
export class VevoService extends BaseService {
  constructor(
    config: ApiConfiguration,
    http: HttpClient
  ) {
    super(config, http);
  }

  /**
   * Path part for operation getVevo
   */
  static readonly GetVevoPath = '/api/vevo/{id}';

  /**
   * Visszaadja a paraméterben kapott azonosítóhoz tartozó vevőt.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to the full `HttpResponse`, allowing access to response headers.
   * To access only the response body, use `getVevo()` instead.
   *
   * This method doesn't expect any request body.
   */
  getVevo$Response(params: {
    id: number;
    context?: HttpContext
  }
): Observable<StrictHttpResponse<VevoDto>> {

    const rb = new RequestBuilder(this.rootUrl, VevoService.GetVevoPath, 'get');
    if (params) {
      rb.path('id', params.id, {});
    }

    return this.http.request(rb.build({
      responseType: 'json',
      accept: 'application/json',
      context: params?.context
    })).pipe(
      filter((r: any) => r instanceof HttpResponse),
      map((r: HttpResponse<any>) => {
        return r as StrictHttpResponse<VevoDto>;
      })
    );
  }

  /**
   * Visszaadja a paraméterben kapott azonosítóhoz tartozó vevőt.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to only to the response body.
   * To access the full response (for headers, for example), `getVevo$Response()` instead.
   *
   * This method doesn't expect any request body.
   */
  getVevo(params: {
    id: number;
    context?: HttpContext
  }
): Observable<VevoDto> {

    return this.getVevo$Response(params).pipe(
      map((r: StrictHttpResponse<VevoDto>) => r.body as VevoDto)
    );
  }

  /**
   * Path part for operation updateVevo
   */
  static readonly UpdateVevoPath = '/api/vevo/{id}';

  /**
   * Módosítja a vevőt a paraméterként kapott adatokkal.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to the full `HttpResponse`, allowing access to response headers.
   * To access only the response body, use `updateVevo()` instead.
   *
   * This method sends `application/json` and handles request body of type `application/json`.
   */
  updateVevo$Response(params: {
    id: number;
    context?: HttpContext
    body: VevoDto
  }
): Observable<StrictHttpResponse<VevoDto>> {

    const rb = new RequestBuilder(this.rootUrl, VevoService.UpdateVevoPath, 'put');
    if (params) {
      rb.path('id', params.id, {});
      rb.body(params.body, 'application/json');
    }

    return this.http.request(rb.build({
      responseType: 'json',
      accept: 'application/json',
      context: params?.context
    })).pipe(
      filter((r: any) => r instanceof HttpResponse),
      map((r: HttpResponse<any>) => {
        return r as StrictHttpResponse<VevoDto>;
      })
    );
  }

  /**
   * Módosítja a vevőt a paraméterként kapott adatokkal.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to only to the response body.
   * To access the full response (for headers, for example), `updateVevo$Response()` instead.
   *
   * This method sends `application/json` and handles request body of type `application/json`.
   */
  updateVevo(params: {
    id: number;
    context?: HttpContext
    body: VevoDto
  }
): Observable<VevoDto> {

    return this.updateVevo$Response(params).pipe(
      map((r: StrictHttpResponse<VevoDto>) => r.body as VevoDto)
    );
  }

  /**
   * Path part for operation deleteVevo
   */
  static readonly DeleteVevoPath = '/api/vevo/{id}';

  /**
   * Vevő törlése id alapján.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to the full `HttpResponse`, allowing access to response headers.
   * To access only the response body, use `deleteVevo()` instead.
   *
   * This method doesn't expect any request body.
   */
  deleteVevo$Response(params: {
    id: number;
    context?: HttpContext
  }
): Observable<StrictHttpResponse<void>> {

    const rb = new RequestBuilder(this.rootUrl, VevoService.DeleteVevoPath, 'delete');
    if (params) {
      rb.path('id', params.id, {});
    }

    return this.http.request(rb.build({
      responseType: 'text',
      accept: '*/*',
      context: params?.context
    })).pipe(
      filter((r: any) => r instanceof HttpResponse),
      map((r: HttpResponse<any>) => {
        return (r as HttpResponse<any>).clone({ body: undefined }) as StrictHttpResponse<void>;
      })
    );
  }

  /**
   * Vevő törlése id alapján.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to only to the response body.
   * To access the full response (for headers, for example), `deleteVevo$Response()` instead.
   *
   * This method doesn't expect any request body.
   */
  deleteVevo(params: {
    id: number;
    context?: HttpContext
  }
): Observable<void> {

    return this.deleteVevo$Response(params).pipe(
      map((r: StrictHttpResponse<void>) => r.body as void)
    );
  }

  /**
   * Path part for operation findAllVevo
   */
  static readonly FindAllVevoPath = '/api/vevo';

  /**
   * Visszaadja az összes vevőt.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to the full `HttpResponse`, allowing access to response headers.
   * To access only the response body, use `findAllVevo()` instead.
   *
   * This method doesn't expect any request body.
   */
  findAllVevo$Response(params?: {

    /**
     * Zero-based page index (0..N)
     */
    page?: number;

    /**
     * The size of the page to be returned
     */
    size?: number;

    /**
     * Sorting criteria in the format: property(,asc|desc). Default sort order is ascending. Multiple sort criteria are supported.
     */
    sort?: Array<string>;
    vevoTipus?: 'CEG' | 'MAGANSZEMELY';
    nev?: string;
    telefonszam?: string;
    email?: string;
    adoszam?: string;
    iranyitoszam?: string;
    telepules?: string;
    utca?: string;
    context?: HttpContext
  }
): Observable<StrictHttpResponse<PageVevoDto>> {

    const rb = new RequestBuilder(this.rootUrl, VevoService.FindAllVevoPath, 'get');
    if (params) {
      rb.query('page', params.page, {});
      rb.query('size', params.size, {});
      rb.query('sort', params.sort, {});
      rb.query('vevoTipus', params.vevoTipus, {});
      rb.query('nev', params.nev, {});
      rb.query('telefonszam', params.telefonszam, {});
      rb.query('email', params.email, {});
      rb.query('adoszam', params.adoszam, {});
      rb.query('iranyitoszam', params.iranyitoszam, {});
      rb.query('telepules', params.telepules, {});
      rb.query('utca', params.utca, {});
    }

    return this.http.request(rb.build({
      responseType: 'json',
      accept: 'application/json',
      context: params?.context
    })).pipe(
      filter((r: any) => r instanceof HttpResponse),
      map((r: HttpResponse<any>) => {
        return r as StrictHttpResponse<PageVevoDto>;
      })
    );
  }

  /**
   * Visszaadja az összes vevőt.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to only to the response body.
   * To access the full response (for headers, for example), `findAllVevo$Response()` instead.
   *
   * This method doesn't expect any request body.
   */
  findAllVevo(params?: {

    /**
     * Zero-based page index (0..N)
     */
    page?: number;

    /**
     * The size of the page to be returned
     */
    size?: number;

    /**
     * Sorting criteria in the format: property(,asc|desc). Default sort order is ascending. Multiple sort criteria are supported.
     */
    sort?: Array<string>;
    vevoTipus?: 'CEG' | 'MAGANSZEMELY';
    nev?: string;
    telefonszam?: string;
    email?: string;
    adoszam?: string;
    iranyitoszam?: string;
    telepules?: string;
    utca?: string;
    context?: HttpContext
  }
): Observable<PageVevoDto> {

    return this.findAllVevo$Response(params).pipe(
      map((r: StrictHttpResponse<PageVevoDto>) => r.body as PageVevoDto)
    );
  }

  /**
   * Path part for operation createVevo
   */
  static readonly CreateVevoPath = '/api/vevo';

  /**
   * Elmenti a paraméterként kapott vevőt.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to the full `HttpResponse`, allowing access to response headers.
   * To access only the response body, use `createVevo()` instead.
   *
   * This method sends `application/json` and handles request body of type `application/json`.
   */
  createVevo$Response(params: {
    context?: HttpContext
    body: VevoDto
  }
): Observable<StrictHttpResponse<VevoDto>> {

    const rb = new RequestBuilder(this.rootUrl, VevoService.CreateVevoPath, 'post');
    if (params) {
      rb.body(params.body, 'application/json');
    }

    return this.http.request(rb.build({
      responseType: 'json',
      accept: 'application/json',
      context: params?.context
    })).pipe(
      filter((r: any) => r instanceof HttpResponse),
      map((r: HttpResponse<any>) => {
        return r as StrictHttpResponse<VevoDto>;
      })
    );
  }

  /**
   * Elmenti a paraméterként kapott vevőt.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to only to the response body.
   * To access the full response (for headers, for example), `createVevo$Response()` instead.
   *
   * This method sends `application/json` and handles request body of type `application/json`.
   */
  createVevo(params: {
    context?: HttpContext
    body: VevoDto
  }
): Observable<VevoDto> {

    return this.createVevo$Response(params).pipe(
      map((r: StrictHttpResponse<VevoDto>) => r.body as VevoDto)
    );
  }

  /**
   * Path part for operation listAllVevo
   */
  static readonly ListAllVevoPath = '/api/vevo/list';

  /**
   * Visszaadja az összes vevőt.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to the full `HttpResponse`, allowing access to response headers.
   * To access only the response body, use `listAllVevo()` instead.
   *
   * This method doesn't expect any request body.
   */
  listAllVevo$Response(params?: {
    context?: HttpContext
  }
): Observable<StrictHttpResponse<Array<VevoDto>>> {

    const rb = new RequestBuilder(this.rootUrl, VevoService.ListAllVevoPath, 'get');
    if (params) {
    }

    return this.http.request(rb.build({
      responseType: 'json',
      accept: 'application/json',
      context: params?.context
    })).pipe(
      filter((r: any) => r instanceof HttpResponse),
      map((r: HttpResponse<any>) => {
        return r as StrictHttpResponse<Array<VevoDto>>;
      })
    );
  }

  /**
   * Visszaadja az összes vevőt.
   *
   *
   *
   * This method provides access to only to the response body.
   * To access the full response (for headers, for example), `listAllVevo$Response()` instead.
   *
   * This method doesn't expect any request body.
   */
  listAllVevo(params?: {
    context?: HttpContext
  }
): Observable<Array<VevoDto>> {

    return this.listAllVevo$Response(params).pipe(
      map((r: StrictHttpResponse<Array<VevoDto>>) => r.body as Array<VevoDto>)

  }

}

import {CUSTOM_ELEMENTS_SCHEMA, ErrorHandler, NgModule, Optional, SkipSelf} from '@angular/core';
import {CommonModule} from '@angular/common';
import {HTTP_INTERCEPTORS, HttpClientModule} from '@angular/common/http';
import {MediaMatcher} from '@angular/cdk/layout';
import {NGXLogger} from 'ngx-logger';
import {SpinnerInterceptor} from './interceptors/spinner.interceptor';
import {throwIfAlreadyLoaded} from './guards/module-import.guard';
import {GlobalErrorHandler} from './services/globar-error.handler';
import {MatSnackBar} from "@angular/material/snack-bar";
import {HttpErrorInterceptor} from "./interceptors/http-error.interceptor";

@NgModule({
  imports: [
    CommonModule,
    HttpClientModule
  ],
  declarations: [
  ],
  providers: [
    MediaMatcher,
    {
      provide: HTTP_INTERCEPTORS,
      useClass: SpinnerInterceptor,
      multi: true
    },
    {
      provide: HTTP_INTERCEPTORS,
      useClass: HttpErrorInterceptor,
      multi: true,
      deps: [MatSnackBar]
    },
    {
      provide: ErrorHandler,
      useClass: GlobalErrorHandler
    },
    { provide: NGXLogger, useClass: NGXLogger },
    { provide: 'LOCALSTORAGE', useValue: window.localStorage }
  ],
  exports: [
  ],
  schemas: [CUSTOM_ELEMENTS_SCHEMA]
})
export class CoreModule {
  constructor(@Optional() @SkipSelf() parentModule: CoreModule) {
    throwIfAlreadyLoaded(parentModule, 'CoreModule');
  }
}


====================================================================================================
📄 FILE: src\app\core\guards\module-import.guard.ts

export function throwIfAlreadyLoaded(parentModule: any, moduleName: string) {
  if (parentModule) {
    throw new Error(`${moduleName} has already been loaded. Import Core modules in the AppModule only.`);
  }
}


====================================================================================================
📄 FILE: src\app\core\interceptors\http-error.interceptor.ts
====================================================================================================

import { HttpErrorResponse, HttpEvent, HttpHandler, HttpInterceptor, HttpRequest } from "@angular/common/http";
import { MatSnackBar } from "@angular/material/snack-bar";
import { catchError, map, Observable, throwError } from "rxjs";

export class HttpErrorInterceptor implements HttpInterceptor {

  constructor(private snackService: MatSnackBar) { }

  intercept(req: HttpRequest<any>, next: HttpHandler): Observable<HttpEvent<any>> {
    return next.handle(req).pipe(
      map(req => {
        return req;
      }),
      catchError((error: HttpErrorResponse) => {
        let errorMessage = error.error.description || 'Hiba történt';
        this.snackService.open(errorMessage, undefined, {
          panelClass: ['error-snackbar'],
          duration: 6000
        });
        return throwError("Hiba történt");
      })
    )
  }
}


====================================================================================================
📄 FILE: src\app\core\interceptors\spinner.interceptor.ts
====================================================================================================

import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpInterceptor, HttpResponse } from '@angular/common/http';
import { HttpRequest } from '@angular/common/http';
import { HttpHandler } from '@angular/common/http';
import { HttpEvent } from '@angular/common/http';
import { tap } from 'rxjs/operators';

import { SpinnerService } from './../services/spinner.service';

@Injectable()
export class SpinnerInterceptor implements HttpInterceptor {

    constructor(private spinnerService: SpinnerService) { }

    intercept(req: HttpRequest<any>, next: HttpHandler): Observable<HttpEvent<any>> {

        this.spinnerService.show();

        return next
            .handle(req)
            .pipe(
                tap((event: HttpEvent<any>) => {
                    if (event instanceof HttpResponse) {
                        this.spinnerService.hide();
                    }
                }, (error) => {
                    this.spinnerService.hide();
                })
            );
    }
}


====================================================================================================
📄 FILE: src\app\core\services\globar-error.handler.ts
====================================================================================================

import { ErrorHandler, Injectable, Injector } from '@angular/core';
import { NGXLogger } from 'ngx-logger';

@Injectable()
export class GlobalErrorHandler implements ErrorHandler {


    constructor(private injector: Injector) { }

    handleError(error: Error) {
        // Obtain dependencies at the time of the error
        // This is because the GlobalErrorHandler is registered first
        // which prevents constructor dependency injection
        const logger = this.injector.get(NGXLogger);

        const err = {
            message: error.message ? error.message : error.toString(),
            stack: error.stack ? error.stack : ''
        };

        // Log  the error
        logger.error(err);

        // Re-throw the error
        throw error;
    }
}

import {Injectable} from '@angular/core';
import {MatSnackBar} from '@angular/material/snack-bar';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class NotificationService {

  constructor(private snackBar: MatSnackBar) {
  }

  public open(message: string) {
    this.openSnackBar(message);
  }

  public openSnackBar(message: string) {
    this.snackBar.open(message, '', {
      duration: 5000
    });
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\core\services\spinner.service.ts

import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { BehaviorSubject } from 'rxjs';

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class SpinnerService {

  visibility = new BehaviorSubject(false);

  constructor() {
  }

  show() {
    this.visibility.next(true);
  }

  hide() {
    this.visibility.next(false);
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\custom-material\magyar-paginator.ts

import { MatPaginatorIntl } from '@angular/material/paginator';

const huRangeLabel = (page: number, pageSize: number, length: number) => {
  if (length == 0 || pageSize == 0) {
    return `0 / ${length}`;
  }

  length = Math.max(length, 0);

  const startIndex = page * pageSize;

  // If the start index exceeds the list length, do not try and fix the end index to the end.
  const endIndex =
    startIndex < length
      ? Math.min(startIndex + pageSize, length)
      : startIndex + pageSize;

  return `${startIndex + 1} - ${endIndex} / ${length}`;
};

export function getHungarianPaginatorIntl() {
  const paginatorIntl = new MatPaginatorIntl();

  paginatorIntl.itemsPerPageLabel = 'Oldalanként:';
  paginatorIntl.nextPageLabel = 'Következő oldal';
  paginatorIntl.previousPageLabel = 'Előző oldal';
  paginatorIntl.getRangeLabel = huRangeLabel;

  return paginatorIntl;
}

import { CdkCellDef } from "@angular/cdk/table";
import {Directive, Input, OnInit} from "@angular/core";
import { MatCellDef, MatTableDataSource } from "@angular/material/table";
import { Observable } from "rxjs";

@Directive({
  selector: "[matCellDef]", // same selector as MatCellDef
  providers: [{ provide: CdkCellDef, useExisting: TypeSafeMatCellDef }],
})
export class TypeSafeMatCellDef<T> extends MatCellDef implements OnInit {
  // leveraging syntactic-sugar syntax when we use *matCellDef
  @Input() matCellDefDataSource?:
    | T[]
    | Observable<T[]>
    | MatTableDataSource<T>;

  // ngTemplateContextGuard flag to help with the Language Service
  static ngTemplateContextGuard<T>(
    dir: TypeSafeMatCellDef<T>,
    ctx: unknown
  ): ctx is { $implicit: T; index: number } {
    return true;
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {
  }
}

import { Component, Input, ViewEncapsulation } from '@angular/core';
import { UntypedFormControl } from '@angular/forms';
import { MatCheckboxChange } from '@angular/material/checkbox';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-select-check-all',
  templateUrl: './select-check-all.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./select-check-all.component.css'],
  encapsulation: ViewEncapsulation.None
})
export class SelectCheckAllComponent {
  @Input()
  model: UntypedFormControl = new UntypedFormControl;
  @Input() values = [];
  @Input() text = 'Select All';

  constructor() { }

  isChecked(): boolean {
    return this.model.value && this.values.length
      && this.model.value.length === this.values.length;
  }

  isIndeterminate(): boolean {
    return this.model.value && this.values.length && this.model.value.length
      && this.model.value.length < this.values.length;
  }

  toggleSelection(change: MatCheckboxChange): void {
    if (change.checked) {
      this.model.setValue(this.values);
    } else {
      this.model.setValue([]);
    }
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\features\altalanos-adatok\altalanos-adat-form\altalanos-adat-form.component.ts

import { Component, Inject, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { AbstractControl, FormControl } from "@angular/forms";
import { MAT_DIALOG_DATA } from "@angular/material/dialog";
import { TorzsadatDto } from "../../../api/models/torzsadat-dto";

@Component({
  selector: 'app-altalanos-adat-form',
  templateUrl: './altalanos-adat-form.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./altalanos-adat-form.component.scss']
})
export class AltalanosAdatFormComponent implements OnInit {

  private idList?: string[];

  azonosito = new FormControl('', [(control: AbstractControl) => {
    if (this.idList?.some(id => id == control.value)) {
      return { Unique: false }
    }
    else {
      return null;
    }
  }]);

  public adat: TorzsadatDto = {
    id: 0,
    ar: 0,
    azonosito: '',
    megnevezes: '',
    mertekegyseg: ''
  }

  private originalId?: string;

  constructor(@Inject(MAT_DIALOG_DATA) data: { list: string[], data: TorzsadatDto }) {
    if (data.data) {
      this.adat = data.data
      this.originalId = data.data.azonosito;
    }
    if (data.list) {
      this.idList = data.list
    }
  }
  ngOnInit(): void {

  }

  getIdValidation(): Boolean {
    if (this.idList) {
      return (this.idList?.some(id => id == this.adat.azonosito && id != this.originalId));
    }
    else return false;
  }

  getFormValidation(): boolean {
    return (!this.adat.ar || !this.adat.megnevezes || !this.adat.mertekegyseg)
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\features\altalanos-adatok\altalanos-adat-list\torzsadat-list.component.ts
====================================================================================================

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { MatDialog } from "@angular/material/dialog";
import { TorzsadatDto } from "../../../api/models/torzsadat-dto";
import { TorzsadatService } from "../../../api/services/torzsadat.service";
import { AltalanosAdatFormComponent } from "../altalanos-adat-form/altalanos-adat-form.component";

@Component({
  selector: 'torzsadat-list',
  templateUrl: './torzsadat-list.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./torzsadat-list.component.scss']
})
export class TorzsadatListComponent implements OnInit {
  adatok: Array<TorzsadatDto> = [];
  displayedColumns = ['id', 'megnevezes', 'azonosito', 'ar', 'mertekegyseg', 'akciok'];

  constructor(private torzsadatService: TorzsadatService, private dialog: MatDialog) { }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.getAdatok();
  }

  addAdat() {
    const ref = this.dialog.open(AltalanosAdatFormComponent, { data: { list: this.adatok.map(adat => adat.azonosito || ''), data: null } });
    ref.afterClosed().subscribe((result: false | TorzsadatDto) => {
      if (result) {
        this.torzsadatService.createTorzsadat({ body: result }).subscribe(() => this.getAdatok());
      }
    });
  }
  modifyAdat(cikkcsoport: TorzsadatDto) {
    const ref = this.dialog.open(AltalanosAdatFormComponent, { data: { data: cikkcsoport, list: this.adatok.map(adat => adat.azonosito || '') } });
    ref.afterClosed().subscribe((result: false | TorzsadatDto) => {
      if (result) {
        this.torzsadatService.updateTorzsadat({ id: result.id!, body: result }).subscribe(() => this.getAdatok());
      }
    });
  }

  private getAdatok() {
    this.torzsadatService.findAllTorzsadat().subscribe(res => {
      this.adatok = res;
    })
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\features\arucikk\arucikk-form\arucikk-form.component.ts
====================================================================================================

import {Component, OnInit} from '@angular/core';
import {ActivatedRoute, Router} from "@angular/router";
import {EgyediAdatDto} from 'src/app/api/models';
import {ArucikkItemDto} from "../../../api/models/arucikk-item-dto";
import {CikkcsoportDto} from "../../../api/models/cikkcsoport-dto";
import {ArucikkService} from "../../../api/services/arucikk.service";
import {CikkcsoportService} from "../../../api/services/cikkcsoport.service";
import {NotificationService} from "../../../core/services/notification.service";
import {environment} from 'src/environments/environment';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-arucikk-form',
  templateUrl: './arucikk-form.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./arucikk-form.component.scss']
})
export class ArucikkFormComponent implements OnInit {
  public arucikk: ArucikkItemDto = {
    raktarozott: false,
    szorzo: 1,
    engedmeny: 0,
    eladasiAr: 0,
    beszallitoiHiany: false,
  };
  protected anyagKeplet?: string;
  protected idoKeplet?: string;
  public mode: 'create' | 'update' = 'create';
  public cikkcsoportok: Array<CikkcsoportDto> = [];
  compareFn = (a: ArucikkItemDto, b: ArucikkItemDto) => a.id === b.id;
  protected egyediAdatok: EgyediAdatDto[] = [];

  constructor(private snackBar: NotificationService,
    private route: ActivatedRoute,
    private arucikkService: ArucikkService,
    private cikkcsoportService: CikkcsoportService,
    private router: Router,
  ) {
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.cikkcsoportService.findAllCikkcsoport().subscribe(csoportok => this.cikkcsoportok = csoportok);
    this.route.params.subscribe(params => {
      const id = params['id']
      if (id) {
        this.mode = 'update';
        this.arucikkService.getArucikk({ id }).subscribe(aru => {
          this.arucikk = aru;
          if (!this.arucikk.egyediAdatok) {
            this.arucikk.egyediAdatok = [];
          }
          (this.arucikk.cikkcsoport?.adattipusok || []).forEach(adatTipus => {
            if (!this.arucikk.egyediAdatok?.find(a => a.azonosito === adatTipus.azonosito))
              this.arucikk.egyediAdatok!.push({
                azonosito: adatTipus.azonosito,
                megnevezes: adatTipus.megnevezes,
                mertekegyseg: adatTipus.mertekegyseg,
                ertek: 0
              })
          });
          const anyagKeplet1 = "1.2 * Szélesség * Magasság / 100 / 100 * ASZ * BeszerzésiÁr";
          const anyagKeplet2 = "1.1 * 2 * (Magasság + Szélesség + 4 * KSZ) / 100 * ASZ * BeszerzésiÁr";
          const idoKeplet1 = "(2 * [Szélesség + Magasság] / 100 * Időszorzó + FixIdő) * MSZ";
          const idoKeplet2 = "(2 * [Szélesség + Magasság] / 100 * Időszorzó + FixIdő + KSZ) * MSZ";
          switch (this.arucikk.cikkcsoport?.id) {
            case environment.betetCikkcsoportId:
              this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet1;
              this.anyagKeplet = anyagKeplet2;
              break;
            case environment.paszpartuCikkcsoportId:
              this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet1;
              this.anyagKeplet = anyagKeplet1;
              break;
            case environment.uvegCikkcsoportId:
              this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet1;
              this.anyagKeplet = anyagKeplet1;
              break;
            case environment.kartonCikkcsoport:
              this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet1;
              this.anyagKeplet = anyagKeplet1;
              break;
            case environment.kasirCikkcsoport:
              this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet1;
              this.anyagKeplet = anyagKeplet1;
              break;
            case environment.kepkeretCikkcsoportId:
              this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet2;
              this.anyagKeplet = anyagKeplet2;
              break;
            case environment.egyebDarabCikkcsoportId:
              this.idoKeplet = "-";
              this.anyagKeplet = "BeszerzésiÁr * ASZ * Darabszám";
              break;
            case environment.egyebFolyometerCikkcsoportId:
              this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet1;
              this.anyagKeplet = "-";
              break;
            case environment.egyebPercCikkcsoport:
              this.idoKeplet = "Idő[perc] * Rezsióradíj";
              this.anyagKeplet = "-";
              break;
          }
        });

      } else {
        this.mode = 'create';
      }
    })
  }

  cikkcsoportChanged() {
    if (this.arucikk.cikkcsoport?.adattipusok) {
      this.arucikk.egyediAdatok = []
      this.arucikk.cikkcsoport.adattipusok.forEach(adatTipus => {
        if (this.arucikk.egyediAdatok)
          this.arucikk.egyediAdatok.push({
            azonosito: adatTipus.azonosito,
            megnevezes: adatTipus.megnevezes,
            mertekegyseg: adatTipus.mertekegyseg,
            ertek: 0
          })
      });
    }
    const anyagKeplet1 = "(((1.2 * (Szélesség * Magasság)) / 100) / 100 * ASZ * BeszerzésiÁr)";
    const anyagKeplet2 = "((1.1 * 2 * (Magasság + Szélesség + 4 * KSZ)) / 100) * ASZ * BeszerzésiÁr)";
    const idoKeplet1 = "(((2 * (Szélesség + Magasság) / 100) * Időszorzó + FixIdő) * MSZ)";
    const idoKeplet2 = "(((2 * (Szélesség + Magasság) / 100) * Időszorzó + FixIdő + KSZ) * MSZ)";
    switch (this.arucikk.cikkcsoport?.id) {
      case environment.betetCikkcsoportId:
        this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet1;
        this.anyagKeplet = anyagKeplet2;
        break;
      case environment.paszpartuCikkcsoportId:
        this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet1;
        this.anyagKeplet = anyagKeplet1;
        break;
      case environment.uvegCikkcsoportId:
        this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet1;
        this.anyagKeplet = anyagKeplet1;
        break;
      case environment.kartonCikkcsoport:
        this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet1;
        this.anyagKeplet = anyagKeplet1;
        break;
      case environment.kasirCikkcsoport:
        this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet1;
        this.anyagKeplet = anyagKeplet1;
        break;
      case environment.kepkeretCikkcsoportId:
        this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet2;
        this.anyagKeplet = anyagKeplet2;
        break;
      case environment.egyebDarabCikkcsoportId:
        this.idoKeplet = "-";
        this.anyagKeplet = "(BeszerzésiÁr * ASZ * Darabszám)";
        break;
      case environment.egyebFolyometerCikkcsoportId:
        this.idoKeplet = idoKeplet1;
        this.anyagKeplet = "-";
        break;
      case environment.egyebPercCikkcsoport:
        this.idoKeplet = "Idő[perc] * Rezsióradíj";
        this.anyagKeplet = "-";
        break;
    }
  }

  save() {
    (this.mode === 'create' ?
      this.arucikkService.createArucikk({ body: { ...(this.arucikk as Required<ArucikkItemDto>), cikkcsoportId: this.arucikk.cikkcsoport!.id! } }) :
      this.arucikkService.updateArucikk({
        id: this.arucikk.id!,
        body: { ...(this.arucikk as Required<ArucikkItemDto>), cikkcsoportId: this.arucikk.cikkcsoport!.id! }
      }))
      .subscribe(_ => {
        this.snackBar.open('Sikeres mentés');
        this.router.navigate(['arucikkek'])
      })
  }

  goBack() {
    window.history.back();
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\features\arucikk\arucikk-list\arucikk-list.component.ts
====================================================================================================

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { FormControl, FormGroup } from '@angular/forms';
import { MatDialog } from "@angular/material/dialog";
import { PageEvent } from "@angular/material/paginator";
import { MatSnackBar } from "@angular/material/snack-bar";
import { Sort } from "@angular/material/sort";
import { ArucikkDto } from "../../../api/models/arucikk-dto";
import { CikkcsoportDto } from "../../../api/models/cikkcsoport-dto";
import { PageArucikkItemDto } from "../../../api/models/page-arucikk-item-dto";
import { ArucikkService } from "../../../api/services/arucikk.service";
import { CikkcsoportService } from "../../../api/services/cikkcsoport.service";
import { ConfirmDialogComponent } from "../../../shared/confirm-dialog/confirm-dialog.component";
import { QueryParamService } from 'src/app/shared/services/query-param.service';
import { Pageable } from 'src/app/api/models/pageable';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-arucikk-list',
  templateUrl: './arucikk-list.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./arucikk-list.component.scss']
})
export class ArucikkListComponent implements OnInit {
  public cikkcsoportok: Array<CikkcsoportDto> = [];

  public arucikkek: PageArucikkItemDto = {};
  defaultSort = 'id,asc';
  pageable: Pageable = {
    sort: [this.defaultSort],
    size: 50,
    page: 0
  };
  search: {
    cikkcsoport?: string;
    cikkszam?: string;
    nev?: string;
    hianylistas?: boolean;
  } = {}
  lastSearch = {};
  displayedColumns = ['cikkszam', 'nev', 'cikkcsoport', 'beszerzesiAr', 'szorzo', 'engedmeny',
    'minimumKeszlet', 'raktarozott', 'hianylistas','beszallitoi' ,'eladasiAr', 'aktualisKeszlet', 'akciok'];
  arucikkSelect: string[] = [];
  filteredArucikkek: string[] = [];
  protected cikkszamSearch = new FormGroup({
    cikkszam: new FormControl<string>(''),
  })

  constructor(private arucikkService: ArucikkService,
    private cikkcsoportService: CikkcsoportService,
    private snackService: MatSnackBar,
    private dialog: MatDialog,
    private queryParamService: QueryParamService
  ) {
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.resolveQueryParams();
    this.getCikkek();

    this.cikkcsoportService.findAllCikkcsoport().subscribe(csoportok => this.cikkcsoportok = csoportok);
    this.arucikkService.listAllArucikk().subscribe(arucikkek => {
      arucikkek.forEach(arucikk => {
        this.arucikkSelect.push(arucikk.cikkszam!);
      })
      this.filteredArucikkek = [...this.arucikkSelect];
    })
    this.cikkszamSearch.controls.cikkszam.valueChanges.subscribe(value => {
      if (value) {
        this.filteredArucikkek = this.arucikkSelect.filter(cikk => cikk.toLowerCase().includes(value.toLowerCase()));
        this.search.cikkszam = value;
      }
      else {
        this.filteredArucikkek = [...this.arucikkSelect];
        this.search.cikkszam = undefined;
      }
    })
  }

  page($event: PageEvent) {
    this.pageable.page = $event.pageIndex;
    this.pageable.size = $event.pageSize;
    this.getCikkek();
  }

  resolveQueryParams() {
    const params = {...this.queryParamService.getSnapshotParams()};
    this.pageable.page = +params['page'] || 0;
    this.pageable.size = +params['size'] || 50;
    this.pageable.sort = params['sort'] || [this.defaultSort];
    this.cikkszamSearch.patchValue({ cikkszam: params['cikkszam'] || ''}, {emitEvent: false})

    delete params['page'];
    delete params['size'];
    delete params['sort'];

    this.search = {...params};
    this.lastSearch = {...params};
  }

  resetSearch() {
    this.pageable = {
      sort: [this.defaultSort],
      size: 50,
      page: 0
    };
    this.search = {
      cikkcsoport: undefined,
      cikkszam: undefined,
      nev: undefined,
      hianylistas: undefined,
    };
    this.cikkszamSearch.patchValue({cikkszam: ''});
    this.getCikkek();
  }

  public getCikkek() {
    if (JSON.stringify(this.search) !== JSON.stringify(this.lastSearch)) {
      this.pageable.page = 0;
      this.lastSearch = {...this.search};
    }
    this.queryParamService.updateQueryParams({
      ...this.search,
      ...this.pageable
    })
    this.arucikkService.findAllArucikk({
      page: this.pageable.page,
      size: this.pageable.size,
      sort: this.pageable.sort,
      cikkcsoport: this.search.cikkcsoport,
      nev: this.search.nev,
      hianylistas: this.search.hianylistas,
      cikkszam: this.search.cikkszam
    }).subscribe(result => {
      this.arucikkek = result;
    })
  }

  getExportUrl() {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    if (this.search.cikkcsoport) {
      params.set("cikkcsoport", this.search.cikkcsoport);
    }
    if (this.search.nev) {
      params.set("nev", this.search.nev);
    }
    if (this.search.hianylistas) {
      params.set("hianylistas", String(this.search.hianylistas));
    }
    if (this.search.cikkszam) {
      params.set("cikkszam", this.search.cikkszam);
    }
    return this.arucikkService.rootUrl + ArucikkService.ExportArucikkekPath + "?" + params.toString()
  }

  sortChange($event: Sort) {
    this.pageable.sort = [$event.active + ',' + $event.direction];
    this.getCikkek();
  }

  deleteCikkszamFilter() {
    this.search.cikkszam = undefined;
    this.cikkszamSearch.patchValue({
      cikkszam: undefined,
    });
    this.filteredArucikkek = [...this.arucikkSelect];
  }


  delete(element: ArucikkDto) {
    const ref = this.dialog.open(ConfirmDialogComponent, {
      data: {
        title: 'Megerősítés',
        message: 'Biztosan törli az árucikket?'
      }
    })
    ref.afterClosed().subscribe((result: boolean) => {
      if (result) {
        this.arucikkService.deleteArucikk({ id: element.id! }).subscribe(res => {
          this.snackService.open('Sikeres törlés');
          this.getCikkek();
        })
      }
    })
  }
}

import {NgModule} from '@angular/core';
import {RouterModule, Routes} from '@angular/router';
import {LayoutComponent} from 'src/app/shared/layout/layout.component';
import { CikkcsoportFormComponent } from './cikkcsoport-form/cikkcsoport-form.component';
import {CikkcsoportListComponent} from './cikkcsoport-list/cikkcsoport-list.component';

const routes: Routes = [
  {
    path: '',
    component: LayoutComponent,
    children: [
      { path: '', component: CikkcsoportListComponent },
      { path: 'letrehozas', component: CikkcsoportFormComponent},
      { path: ':id', component: CikkcsoportFormComponent}
    ]
  }
];

@NgModule({
  imports: [RouterModule.forChild(routes)],
  exports: [RouterModule]
})
export class CikkcsoportRoutingModule { }

📄 FILE: src\app\features\cikkcsoport\cikkcsoport-form\cikkcsoport-form.component.ts

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { FormControl, FormGroup, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { AdattipusDto } from 'src/app/api/models';
import { CikkcsoportService } from 'src/app/api/services';
import { CikkcsoportDto } from "../../../api/models/cikkcsoport-dto";

@Component({
  selector: 'app-cikkcsoport-form',
  templateUrl: './cikkcsoport-form.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./cikkcsoport-form.component.scss']
})
export class CikkcsoportFormComponent implements OnInit {

  public cikkcsoport: CikkcsoportDto = {
    id: 0,
    mertekegyseg: '',
    nev: '',
    fixIdo: 0,
    idoSzorzo: 1,
    adattipusok: []
  }
  displayedColumns = ["megnevezes", "mertekegyseg", "kepletAzonosito", "akciok"];
  egyediAdatForm = new FormGroup({
    megnevezes: new FormControl<string>("", [Validators.required]),
    mertekegyseg: new FormControl<string>("", [Validators.required]),
    fixIdo: new FormControl<number>(0, [Validators.required]),
    idoSzorzo: new FormControl<number>(1, [Validators.required]),
    kepletAzonosito: new FormControl<string>("", [Validators.required])
  })
  egyediAdatok: AdattipusDto[] = [];
  formTitle = "Egyedi adat létrehozás";
  pageTitle = "Cikkcsoport létrehozás";
  egyediAdatBevitel = false;
  egyediAdatSzerkesztes = false;

  constructor(private route: ActivatedRoute, private cikkService: CikkcsoportService, private router: Router) { }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.route.params.subscribe(params => {
      const id = params['id']
      if (id) {
        this.cikkService.getCikkcsoport({ id: id }).subscribe(res => {
          this.cikkcsoport = res;
          this.egyediAdatok = res.adattipusok || [];
          this.pageTitle = "Cikkcsoport szerkesztése";
        })
      }
    })
  }

  save() {
    if (this.egyediAdatok.length) this.cikkcsoport.adattipusok = this.egyediAdatok;
    if (this.cikkcsoport.id) {
      this.cikkService.updateCikkcsoport({ id: this.cikkcsoport.id, body: { ...this.cikkcsoport } }).subscribe(res => {
        this.router.navigate(['cikkcsoportok']);
      })
    } else {
      this.cikkService.createCikkcsoport({
        body: {
          nev: this.cikkcsoport.nev,
          mertekegyseg: this.cikkcsoport.mertekegyseg,
          fixIdo: this.cikkcsoport.fixIdo,
          idoSzorzo: this.cikkcsoport.idoSzorzo,
          adattipusok: this.cikkcsoport.adattipusok
        }
      }).subscribe(res => {
        this.router.navigate(['cikkcsoportok']);
      })
    }
  }

  egyediAdatTorles(element: AdattipusDto) {
    this.egyediAdatok = this.egyediAdatok.filter(egyediAdat => egyediAdat !== element);
  }

  egyediAdatModositas(element: AdattipusDto) {
    this.egyediAdatSzerkesztes = true;
    this.egyediAdatBevitel = true;
    this.formTitle = "Egyedi adat módosítása";
    this.egyediAdatTorles(element);
    this.egyediAdatForm.patchValue({
      kepletAzonosito: element.azonosito,
      megnevezes: element.megnevezes,
      mertekegyseg: element.mertekegyseg
    });
  }

  egyediAdatLetrehozas() {
    if (this.egyediAdatForm.controls.megnevezes.value && this.egyediAdatForm.controls.mertekegyseg.value && this.egyediAdatForm.controls.kepletAzonosito.value) {
      this.egyediAdatok.push({
        azonosito: this.egyediAdatForm.controls.kepletAzonosito.value,
        megnevezes: this.egyediAdatForm.controls.megnevezes.value,
        mertekegyseg: this.egyediAdatForm.controls.mertekegyseg.value
      });
      this.egyediAdatBevitel = false;
      this.egyediAdatok = [...this.egyediAdatok];
      this.egyediAdatForm.patchValue({
        kepletAzonosito: "",
        megnevezes: "",
        mertekegyseg: ""
      });
      this.formTitle = "Egyedi adat létrehozása";
    }
  }

  canSave(): Boolean {
    return ((this.cikkcsoport.nev != '') && (this.cikkcsoport.mertekegyseg != ''));
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { Title } from '@angular/platform-browser';

import { MatDialog } from "@angular/material/dialog";
import { NGXLogger } from 'ngx-logger';
import { NotificationService } from 'src/app/core/services/notification.service';
import { CikkcsoportDto } from "../../../api/models";
import { CikkcsoportService } from "../../../api/services/cikkcsoport.service";

@Component({
  selector: 'app-cikkcsoport-list',
  templateUrl: './cikkcsoport-list.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./cikkcsoport-list.component.css']
})
export class CikkcsoportListComponent implements OnInit {

  public cikkek: CikkcsoportDto[] = [];
  public displayedColumns = ['id', 'nev', 'mertekegyseg', 'fixido', 'idoszorzo', 'akciok'];

  constructor(
    private logger: NGXLogger,
    private notificationService: NotificationService,
    private titleService: Title,
    private cikkcsoportService: CikkcsoportService,
    public dialog: MatDialog,
  ) {
  }

  ngOnInit() {
    this.titleService.setTitle('Cikkcsoportok');
    this.logger.log('Cikkcsoport loaded');
    this.getCikkek();
  }

  getCikkek() {
    this.cikkcsoportService.findAllCikkcsoport().subscribe(cikkek => this.cikkek = cikkek)
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\features\dashboard\dashboard-home\dashboard-home.component.ts
====================================================================================================

import {Component, ElementRef, OnInit, ViewChild} from '@angular/core';
import { NotificationService } from 'src/app/core/services/notification.service';
import { Title } from '@angular/platform-browser';
import { NGXLogger } from 'ngx-logger';
import {MathfieldElement} from "mathlive";
import {ComputeEngine} from "@cortex-js/compute-engine";


@Component({
  selector: 'app-dashboard-home',
  templateUrl: './dashboard-home.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./dashboard-home.component.css']
})
export class DashboardHomeComponent implements OnInit {

  constructor(private notificationService: NotificationService,
    private titleService: Title,
    private logger: NGXLogger) {
  }


  public SZ =20;
  public M =30;
  public BT =0;
  public A =2;
  public B =3;
  public M0 =3;
  public formula: string = '(SZ-BT)*M';
  value = '';
  public formulaJson: string = '';
  @ViewChild('mfe') mfe?: ElementRef<MathfieldElement>;
  public freevars: string[] = [];



  public compute():void {
    const mf = this.mfe?.nativeElement.value!;

    const ce = new ComputeEngine({
      latexDictionary:[
        ...ComputeEngine.getLatexDictionary(),
        { name: 'SZ', trigger: ['S', 'Z'] },
        { name: 'BT', trigger: ['B', 'T'] },
        { name: 'M0', trigger: ['M', '0'] },
      ]
    });
    // ce.defineSymbol('SZ', {trigger: ['S', 'Z'] })
    // ce.box('BT').value = this.BT??0;
    this.freevars = ce.parse(mf).freeVars;
    ce.set({
      BT: this.BT,
      SZ: this.SZ,
      M: this.M,
      A: this.A,
      B: this.B,
      M0: this.M0
    })
    this.value = ce.parse(mf).N().valueOf().toString();
    this.formulaJson = ce.parse(mf).toString();
  }
  ngOnInit() {
    new MathfieldElement();
    this.titleService.setTitle('Dashboard');
    this.logger.log('Dashboard loaded');
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\features\keszletezes\bevetelezes\bevetelezes.component.ts
