                <tr mat-row *matRowDef="let row; columns: displayedColumns;"></tr>
            </table>
            <form fxLayout="row" fxLayoutAlign="center center" fxLayoutGap="20px" class="status-container"
                [formGroup]="form">
                <h2>Státusz módosítása:</h2>
                <mat-form-field appearance="fill">
                    <mat-select formControlName="status">
                        <mat-option [value]="'MEGRENDELVE'">
                            Megrendelve
                        </mat-option>
                        <mat-option [value]="'GYARTASBA_ADVA'">
                            Gyártásba adva
                        </mat-option>
                        <mat-option [value]="'ELKESZULT'">
                            Elkészült
                        </mat-option>
                        <mat-option [value]="'SZALLITVA'">
                            Szállítva
                        </mat-option>
                        <mat-option [value]="'SZAMLAZVA'">
                            Szamlazva
                        </mat-option>
                        <mat-option [value]="'FIZETVE'">
                            Fizetve, lezárva
                        </mat-option>
                        <mat-option [value]="'TOROLT'">
                            Törölt
                        </mat-option>
                    </mat-select>
                </mat-form-field>
            </form>
            <div fxLayout="row" fxLayoutAlign="end center" fxLayoutGap="40px">
                <h2>Nettó:</h2>
                <h2 class="nettoBox">{{getNettoOsszeg() | number:'1.0-0'}} <span> Ft</span></h2>
            </div>
            <div fxLayout="row" fxLayoutAlign="end center" fxLayoutGap="40px">
                <h1>Összesen fizetendő:</h1>
                <h1 class="nettoBox">{{getOsszFizetendo() | number:'1.0-0'}}<span> Ft</span></h1>
            </div>
            <div fxLayout="column" fxLayoutGap="10px">
                <button (click)="goBack()" [style.margin-top]="'1rem'" mat-button color="primary"
                    mat-raised-button>Vissza</button>
                <button (click)="save()" [style.margin-top]="'1rem'" mat-button color="primary"
                    mat-raised-button>Mentés</button>
            </div>
        </mat-card>
    </div>
</div>

📄 FILE: src\app\features\megrendeles\rendeles-detail\rendeles-detail.component.ts
====================================================================================================

import {Component, OnInit} from '@angular/core';
import {FormControl, FormGroup, Validators} from '@angular/forms';
import {ActivatedRoute, Router} from '@angular/router';
import {DolgozoDto, MegrendelesElemDetailsDto, MegrendelesKepDetailsDto, MegrendelesKepDto, VevoDto} from 'src/app/api/models';
import {GyartasiLapPdfService, MegrendelesPdfService, MegrendelesService} from 'src/app/api/services';
import {NotificationService} from "../../../core/services/notification.service";
import {MatDialog} from '@angular/material/dialog';
import {KepStatusFormDialogComponent} from '../kep-status-form-dialog/kep-status-form-dialog.component';
import * as moment from "moment/moment";

@Component({
  selector: 'app-rendeles-detail',
  templateUrl: './rendeles-detail.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./rendeles-detail.component.scss']
})
export class RendelesDetailComponent implements OnInit {

  protected tetelek: MegrendelesKepDetailsDto[] = [];
  protected displayedColumns = ["megnevezes", "netto", "darab", "nettoOssz", "afa", "brutto", "munkaido", "megjegyzes", "status", "actions"];
  protected id = 0;

  constructor(private megrendelesService: MegrendelesService,
              private route: ActivatedRoute,
              private noti: NotificationService,
              private router: Router,
              private megrendelesPdfService: MegrendelesPdfService,
              private gyartasiLapPdfService: GyartasiLapPdfService,
              private dialog: MatDialog
  ) {
  }


  public form = new FormGroup({
    fizetesimod: new FormControl<'KESZPENZ' | 'BANKKARTYA' | 'ATUTALAS'>('KESZPENZ', [Validators.required]),
    dolgozo: new FormControl<string | DolgozoDto>('', [Validators.required]),
    datum: new FormControl<string>('', [Validators.required],),
    atvetel: new FormControl<string>('', [Validators.required]),
    felvetel: new FormControl<string>('', [Validators.required]),
    eloleg: new FormControl<number>(0),
    komment: new FormControl<string>(''),
    nev: new FormControl<string | VevoDto>('', [Validators.required]),
    status: new FormControl<'ARAJANLAT' | 'MEGRENDELVE' | 'GYARTASBA_ADVA' | 'ELKESZULT' | 'SZALLITVA' | 'SZAMLAZVA' | 'FIZETVE' | 'TOROLT'>('ARAJANLAT'),
  })

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.route.params.subscribe(params => {
      const id = params["id"];
      this.id = id;
      this.megrendelesService.getMegrendelesDetails({id: id}).subscribe(value => {
        this.form.patchValue({
          atvetel: value.atvetHelye,
          datum: value.hatarido,
          dolgozo: (value.dolgozo?.vezeteknev || '') + " " + (value.dolgozo?.keresztnev || ''),
          felvetel: value.felvetHelye,
          fizetesimod: value.fizetesiMod,
          komment: value.komment,
          nev: value.vevo?.nev,
          status: value.statusz,
          eloleg: value.kifizetve
        })
        this.tetelek = value.kepek!
        this.tetelek = [...this.tetelek];
      })
    })
    this.form.disable();
    this.form.controls.atvetel.enable();
    this.form.controls.felvetel.enable();
    this.form.controls.fizetesimod.enable();
    this.form.controls.datum.enable();
    this.form.controls.eloleg.enable();
    this.form.controls.status.enable();
  }

  getNettoOsszeg(): number {
    let tempOsszeg = 0;
    this.tetelek.forEach(tetel => {
      let tempTetelOsszeg = 0;
      tetel.elemek!.forEach((elem: MegrendelesElemDetailsDto) => {
        tempTetelOsszeg = tempTetelOsszeg + (elem.ar || 0) * (1 - (elem.engedmeny || 0));
      })
      tempTetelOsszeg = tempTetelOsszeg * (tetel.darabszam || 0);
      tempOsszeg = tempOsszeg + tempTetelOsszeg;
    })
    return tempOsszeg;
  }

  getOsszFizetendo(): number {
    let tempOsszeg = this.getNettoOsszeg() * 1.27;
    return tempOsszeg;
  }

  getTetelOsszAr(tetel: MegrendelesKepDetailsDto): number {
    let tempOsszeg = 0;
    tetel.elemek!.forEach(elem => {
      tempOsszeg = tempOsszeg + (elem.ar || 0) * (1 - (elem.engedmeny || 0));
    })
    return tempOsszeg;
  }

  getTetelOsszMunkaido(tetel: MegrendelesKepDto): number {
    let tempOsszido = 0;
    tetel.elemek!.forEach((elem: MegrendelesElemDetailsDto) => {
      tempOsszido = tempOsszido + (elem.munkaido ?? 0);
      if (elem.mertekegyseg === 'perc') {
        tempOsszido = tempOsszido + (elem.mennyiseg ?? 0);
      }
    })
    return tempOsszido;
  }


  save() {
    this.megrendelesService.updateAjanlat({
      id: this.id, body: {
        atvetHelye: this.form.get('atvetel')?.value!,
        felvetHelye: this.form.get('felvetel')?.value!,
        fizetesiMod: this.form.get('fizetesimod')?.value!,
        hatarido: moment(this.form.get('datum')?.value!).format('YYYY-MM-DD'),
        kifizetve: this.form.get('eloleg')?.value!,
        statusz: this.form.get('status')?.value!
      }
    }).subscribe(
      (value) => {
        this.noti.openSnackBar('Sikeres módosítás');
      }
    )
  }

  goBack() {
    window.history.back();
  }

  downloadPdf(): string {
    return this.megrendelesPdfService.rootUrl + MegrendelesPdfService.GenerateMegrendelesPdfPath + '?id=' + this.id;
  }


  downloadGyartasiLap(): string {
    return this.gyartasiLapPdfService.rootUrl + GyartasiLapPdfService.GenerateGyartasiLapPdfPath + '?id=' + this.id;
  }

  editKepStatus(elem: MegrendelesKepDetailsDto) {
    const ref = this.dialog.open(KepStatusFormDialogComponent, {
      data: {
        status: elem.kepStatusz,
        leltariSzam: elem.leltariSzam
      }
    });
    ref.afterClosed().subscribe((result: false | { status: "ATADVA" | "ATADASRA_VAR" | "URES_KERET", leltariSzam: string }) => {
      if (result) {
        this.megrendelesService.updateMegrendelesKepStatusz({
          id: this.id,
          kepId: elem.id!,
          body: {statusz: result.status, leltariSzam: result.leltariSzam}
        })
          .subscribe((res) => {
            this.tetelek = res.kepek!;
            this.noti.openSnackBar("Sikeres státuszváltás")
          })
      }
    });
  }

}

📄 FILE: src\app\features\megrendeles\rendelesek\rendelesek.component.scss
====================================================================================================



====================================================================================================
📄 FILE: src\app\features\megrendeles\rendelesek\rendelesek.component.ts
====================================================================================================

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { FormControl, FormGroup } from '@angular/forms';
import { PageEvent } from '@angular/material/paginator';
import { Sort } from '@angular/material/sort';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { MegrendelesDto, MegrendelesElemDetailsDto, Pageable, PageMegrendelesDto, VevoDto } from 'src/app/api/models';
import { MegrendelesService, VevoService } from 'src/app/api/services';
import * as moment from "moment";
import { QueryParamService } from 'src/app/shared/services/query-param.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-rendelesek',
  templateUrl: './rendelesek.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./rendelesek.component.scss']
})
export class RendelesekComponent implements OnInit {

  public displayedColumns = ["bizonylatszam", "statusz", "osszeg", "fizetve", "munkaido", "vevo", "hatarido", "datum", "atvetel", "akciok"];
  public vevok: VevoDto[] = []
  protected filteredVevok: VevoDto[] = []
  public rendelesek: PageMegrendelesDto = {}
  defaultSort = 'bizonylatszam,desc'
  pageable: Pageable = {
    sort: [this.defaultSort],
    size: 50,
    page: 0
  };
  public search: {
    bizonylatszam?: string,
    status?: 'ARAJANLAT' | 'MEGRENDELVE' | 'GYARTASBA_ADVA' | 'ELKESZULT' | 'SZALLITVA' | 'SZAMLAZVA' | 'FIZETVE' | 'TOROLT',
    vevo?: VevoDto,
    hataridoTol?: string,
    hataridoIg?: string,
  } = {}
  lastSearch = {};

  sumOsszeg = -1;
  sumMunkaido = -1;

  protected vevoForm = new FormGroup({
    vevo: new FormControl<VevoDto | string>(""),
  })

  constructor(
    private megrendelesService: MegrendelesService,
    private vevoService: VevoService,
    private router: Router,
    private queryParamService: QueryParamService
  ) { }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.vevoService.listAllVevo().subscribe(value => {
      this.vevok = value;
      this.resolveQueryParams();
      this.getRendelesek();
    })
    this.vevoForm.controls.vevo.valueChanges.subscribe(value => {
      const name = typeof value === 'string' ? value : value?.nev!;
      this.filteredVevok = this.vevok.filter(vevo => vevo.nev?.toLowerCase().includes(name.toLowerCase()));
    })
  }

  resolveQueryParams() {
    const params = {...this.queryParamService.getSnapshotParams()};
    this.pageable.page = +params['page'] || 0;
    this.pageable.size = +params['size'] || 50;
    this.pageable.sort = params['sort'] || [this.defaultSort];

    delete params['page'];
    delete params['size'];
    delete params['sort'];

    if (params['vevo']) {
      const vevo: VevoDto = this.vevok.find(vevo => vevo.id === +params['vevo'])!;
      this.vevoForm.patchValue({ vevo }, {emitEvent: false})
      this.filteredVevok = [vevo];
      params['vevo'] = vevo;
    }

    if (params['hataridoTol']) {
      params['hataridoTol'] = new Date(params['hataridoTol']) as any;
    }
    if (params['hataridoIg']) {
      params['hataridoIg'] = new Date(params['hataridoIg']) as any
    }

    this.search = {...params};
    this.lastSearch = {...params};
  }

  displayOsszeg(elem: MegrendelesDto): number {
    let osszeg = 0;
    elem.kepek?.forEach(kep => {
      kep.elemek?.forEach(elem => {
        osszeg = osszeg + (elem.engedmenyesBruttoAr || 0) * (elem.darabszam || 1);
      })
    })
    return osszeg;
  }

  displayFizetve(fizetve: boolean): "Fizetve" | "Nincs fizetve" {
    if (fizetve)
      return "Fizetve";
    else
      return "Nincs fizetve";
  }

  displayMunkaido(elem: MegrendelesDto): number {
    let osszIdo = 0;
    elem.kepek?.forEach(kep => {
      kep.elemek?.forEach((elem) => {
        osszIdo = osszIdo + (elem.munkaido || 0);
        if (elem.cikkcsoportNev === 'Egyéb perc modositva') {
          osszIdo = osszIdo + (Math.abs(elem.mennyiseg ?? 0));
        }
      })
    })
    return osszIdo;
  }

  displayVevo = (v: VevoDto, detail = false) => {
    if (detail) {
      return v ? v.nev + ' - ' + (v.cim?.iranyitoszam || '') + ' ' + (v.cim?.telepules || '') + ' ' + (v.cim?.utca || '') : ""
    }
    else {
      return v ? v.nev : ""
    }
  };

  resetSearch() {
    this.pageable = {
      sort: [this.defaultSort],
      size: 50,
      page: 0
    };
    this.search = {
      bizonylatszam: undefined,
      status: undefined,
      vevo: undefined,
      hataridoTol: undefined,
      hataridoIg: undefined,
    };
    this.vevoForm.patchValue({vevo: ''});
    this.getRendelesek();
  }

  getRendelesek() {
    if (JSON.stringify(this.search) !== JSON.stringify(this.lastSearch)) {
      this.pageable.page = 0;
      this.lastSearch = {...this.search};
    }
    this.queryParamService.updateQueryParams({
      ...this.search,
      vevo: this.search.vevo?.id,
      ...this.pageable
    })

    this.megrendelesService.findAllMegrendeles({
      page: this.pageable.page,
      size: this.pageable.size,
      sort: this.pageable.sort,
      vevoId: this.search?.vevo?.id,
      statusz: this.search.status,
      bizonylatszam: this.search.bizonylatszam,
      hataridoTol: this.search.hataridoTol ? moment(this.search.hataridoTol).format('YYYY-MM-DD') : undefined,
            hataridoIg: this.search.hataridoIg ? moment(this.search.hataridoIg).format('YYYY-MM-DD') : undefined,
    }).subscribe(value => {
      this.rendelesek = value;
      this.sumOsszeg = -1;
      this.sumMunkaido = -1;
    })
  }

  sumFilteredRendelesek() {
    this.megrendelesService.findAllMegrendeles({
      page: 0,
      size: 100000,
      sort: this.pageable.sort,
      vevoId: this.search?.vevo?.id,
      statusz: this.search.status,
      bizonylatszam: this.search.bizonylatszam,
      hataridoTol: this.search.hataridoTol ? moment(this.search.hataridoTol).format('YYYY-MM-DD') : undefined,
            hataridoIg: this.search.hataridoIg ? moment(this.search.hataridoIg).format('YYYY-MM-DD') : undefined,
    }).subscribe(rendelesek => {
        let osszIdo = 0;
        let osszeg = 0;
        (rendelesek.content ?? []).forEach(rendeles => {
          rendeles.kepek?.forEach(kep => {
            kep.elemek?.forEach(elem => {
              osszIdo = osszIdo + (elem.munkaido || 0);
              if (elem.cikkcsoportNev === 'Egyéb perc modositva') {
                osszIdo = osszIdo + (Math.abs(elem.mennyiseg ?? 0));
              }
              osszeg = osszeg + (elem.engedmenyesBruttoAr || 0) * (elem.darabszam || 1);
            })
          })
        })
        this.sumMunkaido = osszIdo;
        this.sumOsszeg = osszeg;
    })
  }


  convertOraPerc(time: number): string {
    let hours = Math.floor(time / 60);
    let minutes = time - (hours * 60);
    let text = '';
    if (hours > 0) {
      text = `${hours} óra`;
    }
    if (minutes > 0) {
      text = text + ` ${minutes} perc`;
    }
    return text;
  }

  sortChange($event: Sort) {
    this.pageable.sort = [$event.active + ',' + $event.direction];
    this.getRendelesek();
  }

  page($event: PageEvent) {
    this.pageable.page = $event.pageIndex;
    this.pageable.size = $event.pageSize;
    this.getRendelesek();
  }

  duplicate(megrendeles: MegrendelesDto) {
    localStorage.setItem("duplicatedOrder", JSON.stringify(megrendeles));
    this.router.navigateByUrl("megrendeles/lakossagi_ajanlat");
  }
}

import {NgModule} from '@angular/core';
import {RouterModule, Routes} from '@angular/router';
import {LayoutComponent} from 'src/app/shared/layout/layout.component';
import {TetelFormComponent} from "./tetel-form/tetel-form.component";
import {TetelListComponent} from "./tetel-list/tetel-list.component";
import {TetelcsoportListComponent} from "./tetelcsoport/tetelcsoport-list/tetelcsoport-list.component";
import {SablonListComponent} from "./sablon-list/sablon-list.component";
import {SablonFormComponent} from "./sablon-form/sablon-form.component";

const routes: Routes = [
  {
    path: '',
    component: LayoutComponent,
    children: [
      {path: 'tetelek/letrehozas', component: TetelFormComponent},
      {path: 'tetelek/lista', component: TetelListComponent},
      {path: 'tetelek/:id', component: TetelFormComponent},
      {path: 'tetelcsoportok/lista', component: TetelcsoportListComponent},
      {path: 'lista', component: SablonListComponent},
      {path: 'letrehozas', component: SablonFormComponent},
      {path: ':id', component: SablonFormComponent},
    ]
  }
];

@NgModule({
  imports: [RouterModule.forChild(routes)],
  exports: [RouterModule]
})
export class SablonokRoutingModule {
}

📄 FILE: src\app\features\sablonok\sablon-form\sablon-form.component.ts
====================================================================================================

import { Component, OnDestroy, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { MatSnackBar } from '@angular/material/snack-bar';
import { ActivatedRoute, Params, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { SablonService } from '../../../api/services/sablon.service';
import { TetelService } from '../../../api/services/tetel.service';
import { TetelcsoportDto } from '../../../api/models/tetelcsoport-dto';
import { TetelItemDto } from '../../../api/models/tetel-item-dto';
import { SablonDto } from '../../../api/models/sablon-dto';
import { ArucikkService } from '../../../api/services/arucikk.service';
import { environment } from '../../../../environments/environment';
import { ArucikkItemDto } from '../../../api/models/arucikk-item-dto';
import { FormControl, FormGroup, Validators } from '@angular/forms';
import { Subscription } from 'rxjs';
import { SablonTetelItemDto } from 'src/app/api/models';

type TetelCsoportWrapper = {
  csoport: TetelcsoportDto;
  tetelek: TetelItemDto[];
};

@Component({
  selector: 'app-sablon-form',
  templateUrl: './sablon-form.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./sablon-form.component.scss'],
})
export class SablonFormComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {
  constructor(
    private snack: MatSnackBar,
    private route: ActivatedRoute,
    private router: Router,
    private sablonService: SablonService,
    private tetelService: TetelService,
    private arucikkService: ArucikkService
  ) {}

  mode: 'create' | 'update' = 'create';
  copyMode = false;

  subs = new Subscription();

  protected form = new FormGroup({
    nev: new FormControl<string>('', Validators.required),
    alaplec: new FormControl<ArucikkItemDto | string>('', Validators.required),
  });

  sablon: SablonDto = {
    sablonTetelek: [],
    nev: '',
    alaplec: {},
  };
  alaplecek: ArucikkItemDto[] = [];
  csoportok: TetelCsoportWrapper[] = [];
  filteredAlaplecek: ArucikkItemDto[] = [];

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.subs.add(
      this.route.params.subscribe((params) => {
        const id = params['id'];
        if (id) {
          this.mode = 'update';
          this.sablonService.getSablon({ id }).subscribe((sablon) => {
            this.sablon = sablon;
            this.form.controls.alaplec.patchValue(this.sablon.alaplec);
            this.form.controls.nev.patchValue(this.sablon.nev);
          });
        } else {
          this.mode = 'create';
        }
      })
    );

    this.subs.add(
      this.route.queryParams.subscribe((params: Params) => {
        const id = params['copy'];
        if (id) {
          this.copyMode = true;
          this.sablonService.getSablon({ id }).subscribe((sablon) => {
            this.sablon = sablon;
            this.sablon.nev = '';
            this.form.controls.alaplec.patchValue(this.sablon.alaplec);
          });
        }
      })
    );

    this.arucikkService.listAllArucikk().subscribe((cikkek) => {
      this.alaplecek = cikkek.filter(
        (c) => c.cikkcsoport?.id === environment.kepkeretCikkcsoportId
      );
      this.filteredAlaplecek = [...this.alaplecek];
    });
    this.tetelService.listAllTetel().subscribe((tetelek) => {
      tetelek.forEach((tetel) => {
        let tetelcsoport: TetelCsoportWrapper | undefined = this.csoportok.find(
          (cs) => cs.csoport.id === tetel.tetelcsoport?.id
        );
        if (!tetelcsoport) {
          tetelcsoport = { csoport: tetel.tetelcsoport!, tetelek: [] };
          this.csoportok.push(tetelcsoport);
        }
        tetelcsoport.tetelek.push(tetel);
      });
      this.csoportok.sort((a, b) =>
        a.csoport.sorrend! < b.csoport.sorrend! ? -1 : 1
      );
    });

    this.form.controls.alaplec.valueChanges.subscribe((value) => {
      const name = typeof value === 'string' ? value : value?.nev!;
      this.filteredAlaplecek = this.alaplecek.filter(
        (cikk) =>
          cikk.nev?.toLowerCase().includes(name?.toLowerCase()) ||
          cikk.cikkszam?.toLowerCase().includes(name.toLowerCase())
      );
      this.filteredAlaplecek = [...this.filteredAlaplecek];
    });
  }

  getActiveForCsoport(csoportId: number): SablonTetelItemDto[] {
    return this.sablon.sablonTetelek?.filter((t) => t.tetelItemDto.tetelcsoport?.id === csoportId);
  }

  getAllForCsoport(csoportId: number): TetelItemDto[] {
    return this.getCsoportById(csoportId).tetelek;
  }

  getCsoportById(csoportId: number): TetelCsoportWrapper {
    return this.csoportok.find((cs) => cs.csoport.id === csoportId)!;
  }

  removeItem(tetelToRemove: SablonTetelItemDto): void {
    const index = this.sablon.sablonTetelek.indexOf(tetelToRemove);
    if (index !== -1) {
      this.sablon.sablonTetelek.splice(index, 1);
    }
  }

  addTetel(tetel: TetelItemDto) {
    const sablonTetel: SablonTetelItemDto = {
      tetelItemDto: tetel,
      idoSzorzo: 1
    }
    this.sablon.sablonTetelek.push(sablonTetel);
  }

  mentes() {
    this.sablon.alaplec = this.form.controls.alaplec
    .value! as ArucikkItemDto;
    this.sablon.nev = this.form.controls.nev.value!;
    if (this.mode === 'create') {
      this.sablonService
      .createSablon({ body: this.sablon })
      .subscribe(() => this.router.navigate(['sablonok', 'lista']));
    } else {
      this.sablonService
      .updateSablon({
        id: this.sablon.id!,
        body: this.sablon,
      })
      .subscribe(() => this.router.navigate(['sablonok', 'lista']));
    }
  }

  displayAlaplec(alaplec: ArucikkItemDto) {
    return alaplec ? alaplec.nev + ' - ' + alaplec.cikkszam : '';
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.subs.unsubscribe();
  }
}

<div class="container" fxLayout="row" fxLayoutAlign="center none">

        <mat-icon>add</mat-icon>

    </div>

            </ng-container>
            <ng-container matColumnDef="alaplec">
              <th mat-header-cell mat-sort-header="alaplec" *matHeaderCellDef> Alapléc</th>
              <td mat-cell *matCellDef="let element; dataSource: sablonok.content!"> {{element.alaplec.nev}}
                - {{element.alaplec.cikkszam}} </td>
            </ng-container>
            <ng-container matColumnDef="akciok" stickyEnd>
              <th mat-header-cell *matHeaderCellDef></th>
              <td mat-cell *matCellDef="let element">
                <div fxLayout="row" fxLayoutAlign="end">
                  <a [routerLink]="'../'+element.id" mat-icon-button color="accent" aria-label="Módosítás">
                    <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                  </a>
                  <a routerLink="/sablonok/letrehozas" [queryParams]="{ copy: element.id }" mat-icon-button>
                    <mat-icon>content_copy</mat-icon>
                  </a>
                  <button (click)="delete(element)" mat-icon-button color="warn" aria-label="Törlés">
                    <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </td>
            </ng-container>
            <tr mat-header-row *matHeaderRowDef="displayedColumns"></tr>
            <tr mat-row *matRowDef="let row; columns: displayedColumns;"></tr>
          </table>
        </div>
        <mat-paginator [pageSizeOptions]="[5, 10, 20, 50, 100]"
                       [length]="sablonok.totalElements || 0"
                       [pageIndex]="sablonok.number || 0"
                       [pageSize]="sablonok.size || 50"
                       (page)="page($event)"
                       showFirstLastButtons
                       aria-label="Select page of periodic elements">
        </mat-paginator>
      </mat-card-content>
    </mat-card>

📄 FILE: src\app\features\sablonok\sablon-list\sablon-list.component.ts

import {Component, OnInit} from '@angular/core';
import {SablonService} from "../../../api/services/sablon.service";
import {MatSnackBar} from "@angular/material/snack-bar";
import {MatDialog} from "@angular/material/dialog";
import {PageEvent} from "@angular/material/paginator";
import {Sort} from "@angular/material/sort";
import {SablonDto} from "../../../api/models/sablon-dto";
import {ConfirmDialogComponent} from "../../../shared/confirm-dialog/confirm-dialog.component";
import {PageSablonDto} from "../../../api/models/page-sablon-dto";
import {ArucikkService} from "../../../api/services/arucikk.service";
import {ArucikkItemDto} from "../../../api/models/arucikk-item-dto";
import {environment} from "../../../../environments/environment";
import {FormControl, FormGroup} from "@angular/forms";
import { QueryParamService } from 'src/app/shared/services/query-param.service';
import { Pageable } from 'src/app/api/models/pageable';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-sablon-list',
  templateUrl: './sablon-list.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./sablon-list.component.scss']
})
export class SablonListComponent implements OnInit {

  protected alaplecForm = new FormGroup({
    alaplec: new FormControl<ArucikkItemDto | string>(''),
  });

  public alaplecek: Array<ArucikkItemDto> = [];
  protected filteredAlaplecek: ArucikkItemDto[] = [];
  public sablonok: PageSablonDto = {};

  defaultSort = 'id,asc'
  pageable: Pageable = {
    sort: [this.defaultSort],
    size: 50,
    page: 0
  }

  search: {
    alaplecId?: number;
    nev?: string;
  } = {}
  lastSearch = {};

  displayedColumns = ['nev', 'alaplec', 'akciok'];

  constructor(private sablonService: SablonService,
              private arucikkService: ArucikkService,
              private snackService: MatSnackBar,
              private dialog: MatDialog,
              private queryParamService: QueryParamService
  ) {
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.arucikkService.listAllArucikk().subscribe(arucikkek => {
      this.alaplecek = arucikkek.filter(
        c => c.cikkcsoport?.id === environment.kepkeretCikkcsoportId
      );
      this.filteredAlaplecek = this.alaplecek;
      this.resolveQueryParams();
      this.getSablonok();
    });
    this.alaplecForm.controls.alaplec.valueChanges.subscribe(value => {
      const name = typeof value === 'string' ? value : value?.nev!;
      this.filteredAlaplecek = this.alaplecek.filter(cikk => cikk.nev?.toLowerCase().includes(name?.toLowerCase()) || cikk.cikkszam?.toLowerCase().includes(name.toLowerCase()));
      this.filteredAlaplecek = [...this.filteredAlaplecek];
      if (typeof value === 'object') {
        this.search.alaplecId = value?.id;
      }
      if (value === '') {
        this.search.alaplecId = undefined;
      }
    })
  }

  resolveQueryParams() {
    const params = {...this.queryParamService.getSnapshotParams()};
    this.pageable.page = +params['page'] || 0;
    this.pageable.size = +params['size'] || 50;
    this.pageable.sort = params['sort'] || [this.defaultSort];

    delete params['page'];
    delete params['size'];
    delete params['sort'];

    if (params['alaplecId']) {
      const alaplec: ArucikkItemDto = this.alaplecek.find(lec => lec.id === +params['alaplecId'])!;
      this.alaplecForm.patchValue({ alaplec }, {emitEvent: false})
      this.filteredAlaplecek = [alaplec];
    }

    this.search = {...params};
    this.lastSearch = {...params};
  }


  page($event: PageEvent) {
    this.pageable.page = $event.pageIndex;
    this.pageable.size = $event.pageSize;
    this.getSablonok();
  }

  resetSearch() {
    this.pageable = {
      sort: [this.defaultSort],
      size: 50,
      page: 0
    };
    this.search = {
      alaplecId: undefined,
      nev: undefined
    };
    this.alaplecForm.patchValue({alaplec: ''});
    this.getSablonok();
  }

  public getSablonok() {
    if (JSON.stringify(this.search) !== JSON.stringify(this.lastSearch)) {
      this.pageable.page = 0;
      this.lastSearch = {...this.search};
    }
    this.queryParamService.updateQueryParams({
      ...this.search,
      ...this.pageable
    })

    this.sablonService.findAllSablon({
      page: this.pageable.page,
      size: this.pageable.size,
      sort: this.pageable.sort,
      alaplecId: this.search.alaplecId,
      nev: this.search.nev,
    }).subscribe(result => {
      this.sablonok = result;
    })
  }

  sortChange($event: Sort) {
    this.pageable.sort = [$event.active + ',' + $event.direction];
    this.getSablonok();
  }

  delete(element: SablonDto) {
    const ref = this.dialog.open(ConfirmDialogComponent, {
      data: {
        title: 'Megerősítés',
        message: 'Biztosan törli az árusablonet?'
      }
    })
    ref.afterClosed().subscribe((result: boolean) => {
      if (result) {
        this.sablonService.deleteSablon({id: element.id!}).subscribe(res => {
          this.snackService.open('Sikeres törlés');
          this.getSablonok();
        })
      }
    })
  }

  displayAlaplec(alaplec: ArucikkItemDto) {
    return alaplec ? alaplec.nev + ' - ' + alaplec.cikkszam : '';
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\features\sablonok\tetel-form\tetel-form.component.ts
====================================================================================================

import {Component, ElementRef, OnInit, ViewChild} from '@angular/core';
import {TorzsadatService} from "../../../api/services/torzsadat.service";
import {TorzsadatDto} from "../../../api/models/torzsadat-dto";
import {megrendelesAdat} from "../../../shared/model/formula-valtozok";
import {Parameter} from "../../../shared/model/parameter";
import {ComputeEngine} from "@cortex-js/compute-engine";
import {MathfieldElement} from "mathlive";
import {TetelcsoportService} from "../../../api/services/tetelcsoport.service";
import {TetelService} from "../../../api/services/tetel.service";
import {TetelcsoportDto} from "../../../api/models/tetelcsoport-dto";
import {ActivatedRoute, Router} from "@angular/router";
import {TetelItemDto} from "../../../api/models/tetel-item-dto";
import {MatSnackBar} from "@angular/material/snack-bar";
import {ArucikkService} from "../../../api/services/arucikk.service";
import {CikkcsoportService} from "../../../api/services/cikkcsoport.service";
import {environment} from "../../../../environments/environment";
import {forkJoin} from "rxjs";
import {EgyediKepkeretService} from "../../../shared/services/egyedi-kepkeret.service";

@Component({
  selector: 'app-tetel-form',
  templateUrl: './tetel-form.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./tetel-form.component.scss']
})
export class TetelFormComponent implements OnInit {
  tetel: TetelItemDto = {
    nev:'',
    arKeplet: '',
    idoKeplet: '',
    tetelcsoport: {
      nev: ''
    }
  }
  tetelcsoportok: Array<TetelcsoportDto> = [];
  adat: Array<TorzsadatDto> = [];
  ce?: ComputeEngine;
  @ViewChild('arformula') arformula?: ElementRef<MathfieldElement>;
  @ViewChild('idoformula') idoformula?: ElementRef<MathfieldElement>;
  vars: { [p: string]: number } = {};
  availableParams: Parameter[] = [];
  unknownParams: string[] = [];
  torzsadatok: Array<TorzsadatDto> = [];
  cikkadatok: Parameter[] = [];
  megrendelesAdat: Parameter[] =[];
  nev = '';
  szamoltAr?: any;
  szamoltIdo?: any;
  private mode: 'create' | 'update' = "create";
  compareFn = (a: TetelcsoportDto, b: TetelcsoportDto) => a.id === b.id;
  loaded: boolean = false;
  get variables(): {key: string, value: number}[] {
    return Object.keys(this.vars).map(key => ({key: key, value: this.vars[key]}));
  }

  constructor(private torzsadatService: TorzsadatService,
              private tetelcsoportService: TetelcsoportService,
              private tetelService: TetelService,
              private cikkcsoportService: CikkcsoportService,
              private route: ActivatedRoute,
              private router: Router,
              private snackBar: MatSnackBar,
              private egyediKepkeretService: EgyediKepkeretService
              ) {
    new MathfieldElement();
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    forkJoin([
        this.torzsadatService.findAllTorzsadat(),
        this.cikkcsoportService.getCikkcsoport({id: environment.kepkeretCikkcsoportId}),
      ]
    ).subscribe(res => {
      this.torzsadatok = res[0];
      this.megrendelesAdat = megrendelesAdat;
      const params = [...res[1].adattipusok||[], ...megrendelesAdat, ...this.torzsadatok]
      this.initComputeEngine();
      this.availableParams = params;
      this.adat = res[0];
      this.updateFreevars();
      this.route.params.subscribe(params => {
        const id = params['id']
        if (id) {
          this.mode = 'update';
          this.tetelService.getTetel({id}).subscribe(tetel =>  {
            this.tetel = tetel;
            this.loaded = true;
            setTimeout(() => this.updateFreevars());
          });
        } else {
          this.loaded = true;
          this.mode = 'create';
        }
      })
    })
    this.cikkcsoportService.getCikkcsoport({id: environment.kepkeretCikkcsoportId}).subscribe(cikkcsoport =>{
      this.cikkadatok = cikkcsoport.adattipusok || [];
    })
    this.tetelcsoportService.findAllTetelcsoport().subscribe(tcs => this.tetelcsoportok = tcs);
  }

  initComputeEngine() {
    const params = [...this.cikkadatok, ...megrendelesAdat, ...this.torzsadatok]
    this.ce = new ComputeEngine({
      ids: [...ComputeEngine.getStandardLibrary()],
      // latexDictiionaryba definiáljuk a saját változóinkat
      latexDictionary:[
        ...ComputeEngine.getLatexDictionary("all"),
        ...params.map(param => ({name: param.azonosito, trigger: param.azonosito.split('')}))
      ]
    });
    this.ce.strict = false;
  }

  updateFreevars() {
    const vars: {[key: string]: number} = {};
    this.initComputeEngine();
    const arForm = this.arformula?.nativeElement.value!;
    this.tetel.arKeplet = arForm;
    this.tetel.arKepletValtozok = this.ce!.parse(arForm)?.freeVars || [];
    this.tetel.arKepletValtozok.forEach(freeVar => vars[freeVar] = this.vars[freeVar]??0);

    const idoForm = this.idoformula?.nativeElement.value!;
    this.tetel.idoKeplet = idoForm;
    this.tetel.idoKepletValtozok = this.ce!.parse(idoForm)?.freeVars || [];
    this.tetel.idoKepletValtozok.forEach(freeVar => vars[freeVar] = this.vars[freeVar]??0);
    this.adat.forEach(torzs => {
      if (vars.hasOwnProperty(torzs.azonosito)) {
        if (vars[torzs.azonosito] === 0){
          vars[torzs.azonosito] = torzs.ar;
        }
      }
    })
    this.vars = vars;
    this.unknownParams = Object.keys(vars).filter(freevar => !this.availableParams.find(par => par.azonosito === freevar ));
    this.calculate();
  }
  calculate() {
    const af = this.arformula?.nativeElement.value!;
    const iform = this.idoformula?.nativeElement.value!;
    this.ce!.set(this.vars);
    if (iform) {
      this.szamoltIdo = parseFloat(this.ce!.parse(iform).evaluate().N().numericValue!.toString());
    }
    if (af) {
      this.szamoltAr = parseFloat(this.ce!.parse(af)?.evaluate().N().numericValue!.toString());
    }
  }

  inUse(azonosito: string) {
    return this.vars.hasOwnProperty(azonosito);
  }

  setValue(key: string, $event: Event) {
    this.vars[key] = parseInt(($event.target as HTMLInputElement).value);
    this.updateFreevars();
  }

    save() {
      (this.mode === 'create' ?
        this.tetelService.createTetel({body: {...(this.tetel as Required<TetelItemDto>) , tetelcsoportId: this.tetel.tetelcsoport!.id!}}) :
        this.tetelService.updateTetel({
          id: this.tetel.id!,
          body: {
            nev: this.tetel.nev!,
            arKeplet: this.tetel.arKeplet!,
            idoKeplet: this.tetel.idoKeplet!,
            arKepletValtozok: this.tetel.arKepletValtozok,
            idoKepletValtozok: this.tetel.idoKepletValtozok,
            tetelcsoportId: this.tetel.tetelcsoport!.id!
          }
        }))
        .subscribe(result => {
          this.snackBar.open('Sikeres mentés', undefined, {duration: 3000});
          this.router.navigate(['/sablonok/tetelek/lista'])
        })

  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\features\sablonok\tetel-list\tetel-list.component.ts

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import {TetelcsoportDto} from "../../../api/models/tetelcsoport-dto";
import {PageTetelItemDto} from "../../../api/models/page-tetel-item-dto";
import {TetelService} from "../../../api/services/tetel.service";
import {TetelcsoportService} from "../../../api/services/tetelcsoport.service";
import {MatSnackBar} from "@angular/material/snack-bar";
import {MatDialog} from "@angular/material/dialog";
import {PageEvent} from "@angular/material/paginator";
import {Sort} from "@angular/material/sort";
import {TetelDto} from "../../../api/models/tetel-dto";
import {ConfirmDialogComponent} from "../../../shared/confirm-dialog/confirm-dialog.component";
import { Pageable } from 'src/app/api/models/pageable';
import { QueryParamService } from 'src/app/shared/services/query-param.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-tetel-list',
  templateUrl: './tetel-list.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./tetel-list.component.scss']
})
export class TetelListComponent implements OnInit {
  public tetelcsoportok: Array<TetelcsoportDto> = [];

  public tetelek: PageTetelItemDto = {};
  defaultSort = 'id,asc';
  pageable: Pageable = {
    sort: [this.defaultSort],
    size: 50,
    page: 0
  };

  search: {    tetelcsoport?: string;
    tetelszam?: string;
    nev?: string;
  } = {}
  lastSearch = {};
  displayedColumns = ['nev', 'tetelcsoport', 'akciok'];

  constructor(private tetelService: TetelService,
              private tetelcsoportService: TetelcsoportService,
              private snackService: MatSnackBar,
              private dialog: MatDialog,
              private queryParamService: QueryParamService
  ) {
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.tetelcsoportService.findAllTetelcsoport().subscribe(csoportok => {
      this.tetelcsoportok = csoportok;
      this.resolveQueryParams();
      this.getTetelek();
    });
  }

  resolveQueryParams() {
    const params = {...this.queryParamService.getSnapshotParams()};
    this.pageable.page = +params['page'] || 0;
    this.pageable.size = +params['size'] || 50;
    this.pageable.sort = params['sort'] || [this.defaultSort];

    delete params['page'];
    delete params['size'];
    delete params['sort'];

    this.search = {...params};
    this.lastSearch = {...params};
  }

  page($event: PageEvent) {
    this.pageable.page = $event.pageIndex;
    this.pageable.size = $event.pageSize;
    this.getTetelek();
  }

  resetSearch() {
    this.pageable = {
      sort: [this.defaultSort],
      size: 50,
      page: 0
    };
    this.search = {
      tetelcsoport: undefined,
      tetelszam: undefined,
      nev: undefined,
    };
    this.getTetelek();
  }

  public getTetelek() {
    if (JSON.stringify(this.search) !== JSON.stringify(this.lastSearch)) {
      this.pageable.page = 0;
      this.lastSearch = {...this.search};
    }
    this.queryParamService.updateQueryParams({
      ...this.search,
      ...this.pageable
    })

    this.tetelService.findAllTetel({
      page: this.pageable.page,
      size: this.pageable.size,
      sort: this.pageable.sort,
      tetelcsoport: this.search.tetelcsoport,
      nev: this.search.nev,
    }).subscribe(result => {
      this.tetelek = result;
    })
  }

  sortChange($event: Sort) {
    this.pageable.sort = [$event.active + ',' + $event.direction];
    this.getTetelek();
  }

  delete(element: TetelDto) {
    const ref = this.dialog.open(ConfirmDialogComponent, {
      data: {
        title: 'Megerősítés',
        message: 'Biztosan törli az árutetelet?'
      }
    })
    ref.afterClosed().subscribe((result: boolean) => {
      if (result) {
        this.tetelService.deleteTetel({id: element.id!}).subscribe(res => {
          this.snackService.open('Sikeres törlés');
          this.getTetelek();
        })
      }
    })
  }

}

import {Component, Inject, OnInit} from '@angular/core';
import {MAT_DIALOG_DATA} from "@angular/material/dialog";
import {THREE} from "@angular/cdk/keycodes";
import {TetelcsoportDto} from "../../../../api/models/tetelcsoport-dto";

@Component({
  selector: 'app-tetelcsoport-form',
  templateUrl: './tetelcsoport-form.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./tetelcsoport-form.component.scss']
})
export class TetelcsoportFormComponent {

  public tetelcsoport: TetelcsoportDto = {
    id: 0,
    nev: '',
  }

  constructor(@Inject(MAT_DIALOG_DATA) data: TetelcsoportDto) {
    if (data) {
      this.tetelcsoport = data;
    }
  }

}

.cdk-column-dragHandle {
  flex-grow: 0.1;
  padding-right: 24px;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.cdk-drag-preview {
  background-color: white;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  border-radius: 4px;
  box-shadow: 0 5px 5px -3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2),
  0 8px 10px 1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.14),
  0 3px 14px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12);
}

.cdk-drag-placeholder {
  opacity: 0;
  background-color: white;
  color: white;
}

.cdk-drag-animating {
  transition: transform 250ms cubic-bezier(0, 0, 0.2, 1);
}

.cdk-drop-list-dragging .mat-row:not(.cdk-drag-placeholder) {
  transition: transform 250ms cubic-bezier(0, 0, 0.2, 1);
}

  <div fxFlex="95%">

📄 FILE: src\app\features\sablonok\tetelcsoport\tetelcsoport-list\tetelcsoport-list.component.ts
====================================================================================================

import { Component, OnInit, ViewChild } from '@angular/core';
import { Title } from '@angular/platform-browser';

import { CdkDragDrop, moveItemInArray } from '@angular/cdk/drag-drop';
import { MatDialog } from "@angular/material/dialog";
import { MatTable } from '@angular/material/table';
import { NGXLogger } from 'ngx-logger';
import { NotificationService } from 'src/app/core/services/notification.service';
import { TetelcsoportDto } from "../../../../api/models/tetelcsoport-dto";
import { TetelcsoportService } from "../../../../api/services/tetelcsoport.service";
import { TetelcsoportFormComponent } from "../tetelcsoport-form/tetelcsoport-form.component";

@Component({
  selector: 'app-tetelcsoport-list',
  templateUrl: './tetelcsoport-list.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./tetelcsoport-list.component.css']
})
export class TetelcsoportListComponent implements OnInit {

  public cikkek: TetelcsoportDto[] = [];
  public displayedColumns = ['dragHandle', 'id', 'nev', 'akciok'];

  constructor(
    private logger: NGXLogger,
    private notificationService: NotificationService,
    private titleService: Title,
    private tetelcsoportService: TetelcsoportService,
    public dialog: MatDialog,
  ) {
  }

  ngOnInit() {
    this.titleService.setTitle('Tetelcsoportok');
    this.logger.log('Tetelcsoport loaded');
    this.getCikkek();
  }

  getCikkek() {
    this.tetelcsoportService.findAllTetelcsoport().subscribe(cikkek => {
      let cikkekSorrenddel = cikkek.filter(cikk => cikk.sorrend != undefined);
      let cikkekSorrendNelkul = cikkek.filter(cikk => cikk.sorrend == undefined);
      this.cikkek = cikkekSorrenddel.sort((a, b) => {
        return (a.sorrend || 0) - (b.sorrend || 0);
      })
      cikkekSorrendNelkul.forEach(value => {
        this.cikkek.push(value);
      })
    })
  }

  addTetelcsoport() {
    const ref = this.dialog.open(TetelcsoportFormComponent);
    ref.afterClosed().subscribe((result: false | TetelcsoportDto) => {
      if (result) {
        this.tetelcsoportService.createTetelcsoport({ body: result }).subscribe(() => this.getCikkek());
      }
    });
  }
  modifyTetelcsoport(tetelcsoport: TetelcsoportDto) {
    const ref = this.dialog.open(TetelcsoportFormComponent, { data: { ...tetelcsoport } });
    ref.afterClosed().subscribe((result: false | TetelcsoportDto) => {
      if (result) {
        this.tetelcsoportService.updateTetelcsoport({ id: result.id!, body: result }).subscribe(() => this.getCikkek());
      }
    });
  }

  drop(event: CdkDragDrop<TetelcsoportDto[]>) {
    moveItemInArray(this.cikkek, event.previousIndex, event.currentIndex);
    this.cikkek = [...this.cikkek];
    this.cikkek.forEach((value, index) => {
      value.sorrend = index;
      if (value.id) {
        this.tetelcsoportService.updateTetelcsoport({ id: value.id, body: value }).subscribe()
      }
    })
  }
}

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { Title } from '@angular/platform-browser';

import { NGXLogger } from 'ngx-logger';
import { NotificationService } from 'src/app/core/services/notification.service';
import {DolgozoService} from "../../../api/services/dolgozo.service";
import {DolgozoDto} from "../../../api/models/dolgozo-dto";
import {CikkcsoportFormComponent} from "../../cikkcsoport/cikkcsoport-form/cikkcsoport-form.component";
import {CikkcsoportDto} from "../../../api/models/cikkcsoport-dto";
import {MatDialog} from "@angular/material/dialog";
import {UserFormComponent} from "../user-form/user-form.component";
import {ArucikkDto} from "../../../api/models/arucikk-dto";
import {ConfirmDialogComponent} from "../../../shared/confirm-dialog/confirm-dialog.component";

@Component({
  selector: 'app-user-list',
  templateUrl: './user-list.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./user-list.component.css']
})
export class UserListComponent implements OnInit {
  public dolgozok: Array<DolgozoDto> = [];
  displayedColumns: string[] = ['id', 'vezeteknev', 'keresztnev', 'akciok'];
  constructor(
    private logger: NGXLogger,
    private dolgozoService: DolgozoService,
    private notificationService: NotificationService,
    private titleService: Title,
    private dialog: MatDialog
  ) { }

  ngOnInit() {
    this.titleService.setTitle('Felhasználók');
    this.getDolgozok();
  }

  getDolgozok(){
    this.dolgozoService.findAllDolgozo().subscribe(res => {
      this.dolgozok = res;
      this.logger.log('Users loaded');
    })
  }

  addUser() {
    const ref = this.dialog.open(UserFormComponent);
    ref.afterClosed().subscribe((result: false|DolgozoDto) => {
      if (result) {
        this.dolgozoService.createDolgozo({body:result}).subscribe(() => this.getDolgozok());
      }
    });
  }
  modifyUser(cikkcsoport: DolgozoDto) {
    const ref = this.dialog.open(UserFormComponent, {data: {...cikkcsoport}});
    ref.afterClosed().subscribe((result: false|DolgozoDto) => {
      if (result) {
        this.dolgozoService.updateDolgozo({id: result.id!, body:result}).subscribe(() => this.getDolgozok());
      }
    });
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\features\vevok\vevoform\vevoform.component.ts
====================================================================================================

import {Component, OnInit} from '@angular/core';
import {VevoService} from "../../../api/services/vevo.service";
import {ActivatedRoute, Router} from "@angular/router";
import {VevoDto} from "../../../api/models/vevo-dto";
import {MatSnackBar} from "@angular/material/snack-bar";

@Component({
  selector: 'app-vevoform',
  templateUrl: './vevoform.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./vevoform.component.scss']
})
export class VevoformComponent implements OnInit {
  mode: 'create' | 'update' = 'create';
  vevo: VevoDto = {vevoTipus: "MAGANSZEMELY", nev: '', cim: {}};

  constructor(private vevoService: VevoService, private route: ActivatedRoute, private snackBar: MatSnackBar, private router: Router) {
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.route.params.subscribe(params => {
      const id = params['id']
      if (id) {
        this.mode = 'update';
        this.vevoService.getVevo({id}).subscribe(vevo => {
          this.vevo = vevo;
          this.vevo.engedmeny = (this.vevo.engedmeny || 0 ) * 100
          if(!this.vevo.cim) {
            this.vevo.cim = {};
          }
        });
      } else {
        this.mode = 'create';
      }
    })
  }

  save() {
    if (this.mode === "create") {
      this.vevoService.createVevo({body: {...this.vevo, engedmeny: (this.vevo.engedmeny || 0) /100}}).subscribe( _ => {
        this.snackBar.open('Sikeres mentés','', {
          duration: 3000
        });
        this.mode = 'update';
        this.router.navigate(['vevok/lista']);
      })
    } else {
      this.vevoService.updateVevo({id: this.vevo.id!, body: {...this.vevo, engedmeny: (this.vevo.engedmeny || 0) /100}}).subscribe({
        next: _ => {
          this.snackBar.open('Sikeres mentés','', {
            duration: 3000
          });
          this.router.navigate(['vevok/lista']);
        },
        error: err => {
          this.snackBar.open('Sikertelen mentés!');
        }
      })
    }
  }
}

                 (matSortChange)="sortChange($event)"
                 [dataSource]="vevok.content!">
            <ng-container matColumnDef="nev">
              <th mat-header-cell mat-sort-header="nev" *matHeaderCellDef> Név </th>
              <td mat-cell *matCellDef="let element; dataSource: vevok.content!"> {{element.nev}} </td>
            </ng-container>
            <ng-container matColumnDef="email">
              <th mat-header-cell mat-sort-header="email" *matHeaderCellDef> Email </th>
              <td mat-cell *matCellDef="let element; dataSource: vevok.content!"> {{element.email}} </td>
            </ng-container>
            <ng-container matColumnDef="telefonszam">
              <th mat-header-cell mat-sort-header="telefonszam" *matHeaderCellDef> Telefonszám </th>
              <td mat-cell *matCellDef="let element; dataSource: vevok.content!"> {{element.telefonszam}} </td>
            </ng-container>
            <ng-container matColumnDef="cim">
              <th mat-header-cell mat-sort-header="cim" *matHeaderCellDef> Cím </th>
              <td mat-cell *matCellDef="let element; dataSource: vevok.content!">
                {{element.cim?.iranyitoszam}}
                {{element.cim?.telepules}}
                {{element.cim?.utca}}
              </td>
            </ng-container>
            <ng-container matColumnDef="adoszam">
              <th mat-header-cell mat-sort-header="adoszam" *matHeaderCellDef> Adószám </th>
              <td mat-cell *matCellDef="let element; dataSource: vevok.content!"> {{element.adoszam}} </td>
            </ng-container>
            <ng-container matColumnDef="vevoTipus">
              <th mat-header-cell mat-sort-header="vevoTipus" *matHeaderCellDef> Vevőtípus </th>
              <td mat-cell *matCellDef="let element; dataSource: vevok.content!">
                {{element.vevoTipus === 'CEG' ? 'Cég':'Magánszemély'}}
              </td>
            </ng-container>
            <ng-container matColumnDef="akciok" stickyEnd>
              <th mat-header-cell *matHeaderCellDef></th>
              <td mat-cell *matCellDef="let element">
                <div fxLayout="row" fxLayoutAlign="end">
                  <a [routerLink]="'../adatlap/'+element.id" mat-icon-button color="accent" aria-label="Módosítás">
                    <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                  </a>
                  <button (click)="delete(element)" mat-icon-button color="warn" aria-label="Törlés">
                    <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </td>
            </ng-container>
            <tr mat-header-row *matHeaderRowDef="displayedColumns"></tr>
            <tr mat-row *matRowDef="let row; columns: displayedColumns;"></tr>
          </table>
        </div>

                       [length]="vevok.totalElements"
                       [pageIndex]="vevok.number"
                       [pageSize]="vevok.size"
                       (page)="page($event)"
                       showFirstLastButtons
                       aria-label="Válassza ki a listázni kívánt vevők oldalszámát">
        </mat-paginator>
      </mat-card-content>
    </mat-card>

📄 FILE: src\app\features\vevok\vevolista\vevolista.component.ts
====================================================================================================

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import {VevoService} from "../../../api/services/vevo.service";
import {VevoDto} from "../../../api/models/vevo-dto";
import {PageVevoDto} from "../../../api/models/page-vevo-dto";
import {PageEvent} from "@angular/material/paginator";
import {Sort} from "@angular/material/sort";
import {ConfirmDialogComponent} from "../../../shared/confirm-dialog/confirm-dialog.component";
import {MatDialog} from "@angular/material/dialog";
import {MatSnackBar} from "@angular/material/snack-bar";
import { Pageable } from 'src/app/api/models/pageable';
import { QueryParamService } from 'src/app/shared/services/query-param.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-vevolista',
  templateUrl: './vevolista.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./vevolista.component.scss']
})
export class VevolistaComponent implements OnInit {
  vevok: PageVevoDto = {};
  defaultSort = 'nev,asc';
  pageable: Pageable = {
    sort: [this.defaultSort],
    size: 50,
    page: 0
  };
  search: {
    vevoTipus?: 'CEG' | 'MAGANSZEMELY';
    nev?: string;
    telefonszam?: string;
    email?: string;
    adoszam?: string;
    iranyitoszam?: string;
    telepules?: string;
    utca?: string;
  } = {};
  lastSearch = {};
  displayedColumns: string[] = ['nev', 'cim', 'email', 'telefonszam', 'adoszam', 'vevoTipus', 'akciok'];

  constructor(
    private vevoService: VevoService,
    private dialog: MatDialog,
    private snack: MatSnackBar,
    private queryParamService: QueryParamService
  ) { }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.resolveQueryParams();
    this.getData();
  }

  resolveQueryParams() {
    const params = {...this.queryParamService.getSnapshotParams()};
    this.pageable.page = +params['page'] || 0;
    this.pageable.size = +params['size'] || 50;
    this.pageable.sort = params['sort'] || [this.defaultSort];

    delete params['page'];
    delete params['size'];
    delete params['sort'];

    this.search = {...params};
    this.lastSearch = {...params};
  }

  resetSearch() {
    this.pageable = {
      sort: [this.defaultSort],
      size: 50,
      page: 0
    };
    this.search = {
      vevoTipus: undefined,
      nev: undefined,
      telefonszam: undefined,
      email: undefined,
      adoszam: undefined,
      iranyitoszam: undefined,
      telepules: undefined,
      utca: undefined,
    };
    this.getData();
  }

  getData() {
    if (JSON.stringify(this.search) !== JSON.stringify(this.lastSearch)) {
      this.pageable.page = 0;
      this.lastSearch = {...this.search};
    }
    this.queryParamService.updateQueryParams({
      ...this.search,
      ...this.pageable
    })

    this.vevoService.findAllVevo({
      page: this.pageable.page,
      size: this.pageable.size,
      sort: this.pageable.sort,
      ...this.search
    }).subscribe(vevok =>{
      this.vevok = vevok;
    })
  }
  page($event: PageEvent) {
    this.pageable.page = $event.pageIndex;
    this.pageable.size = $event.pageSize;
    this.getData();
  }

  sortChange($event: Sort) {
    this.pageable.sort = [$event.active + ',' + $event.direction];
    this.getData();
  }

  delete(element: VevoDto) {
    const ref = this.dialog.open(ConfirmDialogComponent, {
      data: {
        title: 'Megerősítés',
        message: 'Biztosan törli az vevőt?'
      }
    })
    ref.afterClosed().subscribe((result: boolean) => {
      if (result) {
        this.vevoService.deleteVevo({id: element.id!}).subscribe(res => {
          this.snack.open('Sikeres törlés');
          this.getData();
        })
      }
    })
  }
}

import { Component, Inject } from '@angular/core';
import { MatDialogRef, MAT_DIALOG_DATA } from '@angular/material/dialog';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-confirm-dialog',
  templateUrl: './confirm-dialog.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./confirm-dialog.component.css']
})
export class ConfirmDialogComponent {
  title: string;
  message: string;

  constructor(public dialogRef: MatDialogRef<ConfirmDialogComponent>,
    @Inject(MAT_DIALOG_DATA) public data: ConfirmDialogModel) {
    this.title = data.title;
    this.message = data.message;
  }

  onConfirm(): void {
    this.dialogRef.close(true);
  }

  onDismiss(): void {
    this.dialogRef.close(false);
  }
}

/**
 * Class to represent confirm dialog model.
 *
 * It has been kept here to keep it as part of shared component.
 */
export class ConfirmDialogModel {

  constructor(public title: string, public message: string) {
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\shared\layout\layout.component.ts

import {AfterViewInit, ChangeDetectorRef, Component, OnDestroy, OnInit} from '@angular/core';
import {MediaMatcher} from '@angular/cdk/layout';

import {SpinnerService} from '../../core/services/spinner.service';

type MenuItem = { icon: string, name: string, link: string[] }
type MenuGroup = { name: string, items: MenuItem[] }

@Component({
  selector: 'app-layout',
  templateUrl: './layout.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./layout.component.css']
})
export class LayoutComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy, AfterViewInit {

  private _mobileQueryListener: () => void;
  mobileQuery: MediaQueryList;
  showSpinner: boolean = false;

  constructor(private changeDetectorRef: ChangeDetectorRef,
              private media: MediaMatcher,
              public spinnerService: SpinnerService,
  ) {

    this.mobileQuery = this.media.matchMedia('(max-width: 1000px)');
    this._mobileQueryListener = () => changeDetectorRef.detectChanges();
    // tslint:disable-next-line: deprecation
    this.mobileQuery.addListener(this._mobileQueryListener);
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {

  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    // tslint:disable-next-line: deprecation
    this.mobileQuery.removeListener(this._mobileQueryListener);
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.changeDetectorRef.detectChanges();
  }
}

import { SpinnerService } from '../../core/services/spinner.service';

export class SpinnerConsumer {
    isBusy = false;

    constructor(private spinnerService: SpinnerService) {
        this.spinnerService.visibility.subscribe((value: boolean) => {
            this.isBusy = value;
        });
    }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\shared\pipes\limit-to.pipe.ts
====================================================================================================

import { Pipe, PipeTransform } from '@angular/core';

@Pipe({
  name: 'limitTo'
})
export class LimitToPipe implements PipeTransform {

  transform(value: string, limitTo: string): string {

    if (value === undefined || value === null) {
      return '';
    }

    const limit = limitTo ? parseInt(limitTo, 10) : 10;
    const trail = '..';

    return value.length > limit ? value.substring(0, limit) + trail : value;
  }

}


====================================================================================================
📄 FILE: src\app\shared\pipes\local-date.pipe.ts
====================================================================================================

import { Pipe, PipeTransform } from '@angular/core';
import * as moment from 'moment';

@Pipe({
  name: 'localDate'
})
export class LocalDatePipe implements PipeTransform {

  transform(value: Date, args: string): string {
    if (!value || !args) {
      return '';
    }
    return moment.utc(value).local().format(args);
  }

}

import { Pipe, PipeTransform } from '@angular/core';
import {DolgozoDto} from "../../api/models/dolgozo-dto";

@Pipe({
  name: 'nev'
})
export class NevPipe implements PipeTransform {

  transform(value?: DolgozoDto): string {
    return value?.vezeteknev + " " + value?.keresztnev;
  }

}

📄 FILE: src\app\shared\pipes\statusz-nev-pipe.ts
====================================================================================================

import { Pipe, PipeTransform } from '@angular/core';
import {DolgozoDto} from "../../api/models/dolgozo-dto";
import {MegrendelesDto} from "../../api/models/megrendeles-dto";

@Pipe({
  name: 'statuszNev'
})
export class StatuszNevPipe implements PipeTransform {

  transform(megrendelesDto?: MegrendelesDto): string {
    let label: string = megrendelesDto?.statusz || '';
    switch (megrendelesDto?.statusz) {
      case 'ARAJANLAT':
        label = 'Árajánlat';
        break;
      case 'MEGRENDELVE':
        label = 'Megrendelve';
        break;
      case 'GYARTASBA_ADVA':
        label = 'Gyártásba adva';
        break;
      case 'ELKESZULT':
        label = 'Elkészült';
        break;
      case 'SZALLITVA':
        label = 'Szállítva';
        break;
      case 'SZAMLAZVA':
        label = 'Számlázva';
        break;
      case 'FIZETVE':
        label = 'Fizetve, lezárva';
        break;
      case 'TOROLT':
        label = 'Törölt';
        break;
    }
    return label;
  }

}

import { Pipe, PipeTransform } from '@angular/core';

@Pipe({
  name: 'yesNo'
})
export class YesNoPipe implements PipeTransform {

  transform(value?: boolean): string {
    return value ? 'Igen' : 'Nem';
  }

}

📄 FILE: src\app\shared\services\egyedi-kepkeret.service.ts
====================================================================================================

import {Injectable} from "@angular/core";
import {CikkcsoportService} from "../../api/services/cikkcsoport.service";
import {environment} from "../../../environments/environment";
import {AdattipusDto} from "../../api/models/adattipus-dto";
import {ComputeEngine} from "@cortex-js/compute-engine";
import {megrendelesAdat} from "../model/formula-valtozok";
import {TorzsadatService} from "../../api/services/torzsadat.service";
import {TorzsadatDto} from "../../api/models/torzsadat-dto";
import {ArucikkItemDto} from "../../api/models/arucikk-item-dto";
import {ReplaySubject} from "rxjs";
import {BoxedExpression} from "@cortex-js/compute-engine/dist/types/compute-engine/public";


@Injectable({providedIn: 'root'})
export class EgyediKepkeretService {
  private cikkadatok: Array<AdattipusDto> = [];
  private torzsadatok: Array<TorzsadatDto> = [];
  public engineReady: ReplaySubject<void> = new ReplaySubject<void>();
  vars: { [key: string]: number } = {};
  constructor(private cikkcsoportService: CikkcsoportService,
              private torzsadatService: TorzsadatService) {
    this.cikkcsoportService.getCikkcsoport({id: environment.kepkeretCikkcsoportId}).subscribe(cikkcsoport =>{
      this.cikkadatok = cikkcsoport.adattipusok || [];
      this.torzsadatService.findAllTorzsadat().subscribe(a => {
        this.torzsadatok = a;
        this.torzsadatok.forEach(torzs => {
          this.vars[torzs.azonosito] = torzs.ar;
        })
        this.engineReady.next();
      });
    })
  }

  createComputeEngine(): ComputeEngine {
    const params = [...this.cikkadatok, ...megrendelesAdat, ...this.torzsadatok]
    const ce = new ComputeEngine({
      ids: [...ComputeEngine.getStandardLibrary()],
      // latexDictiionaryba definiáljuk a saját változóinkat
      latexDictionary:[
        ...ComputeEngine.getLatexDictionary("all"),
        ...params.map(param => ({name: param.azonosito, trigger: param.azonosito.split('')}))
      ]
    });
    ce.strict = false;
    return ce;
  }

  calculate(keplet: string, alaplec: ArucikkItemDto, szelesseg: number, magassag: number): number {
    const lecVars: { [key: string]: number } = {};
    alaplec.egyediAdatok?.forEach(adat => {
      lecVars[adat.azonosito] = adat.ertek;
    })
    const ce = this.createComputeEngine();
    ce.set({
      ...this.vars,
      ...lecVars,
      SZ: this.wrapValueToFixedPrecision(szelesseg, ce),
      M: this.wrapValueToFixedPrecision(magassag, ce)
    });
    return EgyediKepkeretService.toFixed(ce.parse(keplet).evaluate().N().numericValue!.toString())
  }

  wrapValueToFixedPrecision(value: number, ce: ComputeEngine): BoxedExpression {
    return ce.parse(Math.round(value*100).toString() + '\\div 100')
  }

  public static toFixed(x: string | number, decimals = 2) {
    const mult = Math.pow(10, decimals);
    if (typeof x === 'string') {
      return Math.round(parseFloat(x) * mult) / mult;
    }
    return Math.round(x * mult) / mult;
  }
}

📄 FILE: src\app\shared\services\query-param.service.ts
====================================================================================================

// query-param.service.ts
import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { ActivatedRoute, Params, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class QueryParamService {
  constructor(
    private readonly router: Router,
    private readonly route: ActivatedRoute
  ) {}

  getQueryParams(): Observable<Params> {
    return this.route.queryParams;
  }

  getSnapshotParams(): Params {
    return this.route.snapshot.queryParams;
  }

  updateQueryParams(params: Params, merge = true): void {
    const finalParams = params;

    this.router.navigate([], {
      relativeTo: this.route,
      queryParams: finalParams,
      queryParamsHandling: merge ? 'merge' : '',
    });
  }

  removeQueryParams(keys: string[]): void {
    const currentParams = { ...this.route.snapshot.queryParams };
    keys.forEach(k => delete currentParams[k]);
    this.router.navigate([], {
      relativeTo: this.route,
      queryParams: currentParams,
    });
  }

  getParam<T = string>(key: string, fallback: T): T {
    const value = this.route.snapshot.queryParams[key];
    return (value !== undefined ? value : fallback) as T;
  }

}

📄 FILE: src\app\shared\validators\autocompleteSelectionValidator.ts
====================================================================================================

import { AbstractControl } from '@angular/forms';

export function AutocompleteSelectionValidator(control: AbstractControl) {
    const selection = control.value;

    if (selection && typeof selection === 'string') {
        return { incorrect: true };
    }

    return null;
}
